nd enabled == true
		else
			warn("[Record] TenFootInterface failed; using desktop layout: " .. tostring(api))
		end
	else
		warn("[Record] TenFootInterface missing; using desktop layout.")
	end
end

local flagSuccess, flagResult = pcall(function()
	return settings():GetFFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization")
end)
local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization = flagSuccess and flagResult == true

local function Initialize()
	local factoryModule = SettingsFolder:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory", 15)
	assert(factoryModule and factoryModule:IsA("ModuleScript"), "[Record] SettingsPageFactory is missing.")
	local factory = require(factoryModule)
	assert(type(factory) == "table" and type(factory.CreateNewPage) == "function",
		"[Record] SettingsPageFactory.CreateNewPage is missing.")

	local this = factory:CreateNewPage()
	local isRecordingVideo = false
	local recordingEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	recordingEvent.Name = "RecordingEvent"
	this.RecordingChanged = recordingEvent.Event

	local recordButton
	local recordingStateConnections = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local recordSomethingConnection
	local recordingRequestPending = false
	local recordingRequestGeneration = 0
	local pendingRecordingTarget = nil

	local function setButtonCaption(button, captionText)
		if not button then
			return
		end

		if button:IsA("TextButton") or button:IsA("TextLabel") then
			button.Text = captionText
			return
		end

		local candidates = {}
		for _, descendant in ipairs(button:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
				table.insert(candidates, descendant)
			end
		end

		-- Prefer the existing button caption instead of changing unrelated
		-- labels from the row or page.
		for _, label in ipairs(candidates) do
			local name = string.lower(label.Name)
			local text = string.lower(label.Text or "")
			if name:find("text", 1, true)
				or name:find("caption", 1, true)
				or text:find("record", 1, true)
				or text:find("stop", 1, true) then
				label.Text = captionText
				return
			end
		end

		if candidates[1] then
			candidates[1].Text = captionText
		end
	end

	local function updateRecordButtonText()
		setButtonCaption(recordButton, isRecordingVideo and "Stop Recording" or "Record Video")
	end

	local function setRecordingState(active, forceNotify)
		active = active == true
		if recordingRequestPending and pendingRecordingTarget == active then
			recordingRequestPending = false
			pendingRecordingTarget = nil
			recordingRequestGeneration += 1
		end
		local changed = isRecordingVideo ~= active
		isRecordingVideo = active
		updateRecordButtonText()
		if changed or forceNotify then
			recordingEvent:Fire(isRecordingVideo)
		end
	end

	function this:IsRecording()
		return isRecordingVideo
	end

	this.TabHeader.Name = "RecordTab"
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/RecordTab.png"
	local aspect = this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("AspectRatioConstraint")
	if aspect then
		aspect.AspectRatio = 41 / 40
	end

	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		local title = this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if title then title.Text = "Record" end
	else
		local title = this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("Title")
		if title then title.Text = "Record" end
	end

	local function onVREnabled()
		this.TabHeader.Visible = not VRService.VREnabled
	end
	onVREnabled()
	VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(onVREnabled)

	this.Page.Name = "Record"

	local function makeTextLabel(name, text, isTitle, parent, layoutOrder)
		local leftPadding = 10
		local bottomPadding = isTitle and 0 or 10
		local textSize = isTitle and 36 or 24
		local font = isTitle and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans

		local container = utility:Create "Frame" {
			Name = name .. "Container",
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			ZIndex = 2,
			LayoutOrder = layoutOrder,
			Parent = parent,
		}

		local label = utility:Create "TextLabel" {
			Name = name,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = text,
			TextWrapped = true,
			Font = font,
			TextSize = textSize,
			TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
			Position = UDim2.new(0, leftPadding, 0, 0),
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -leftPadding, 1, 0),
			ZIndex = 2,
			Parent = container,
		}

		local function updateSize()
			local width = math.max(1, parent.AbsoluteSize.X - leftPadding - 10)
			local measured = TextService:GetTextSize(
				text,
				label.TextSize,
				label.Font,
				Vector2.new(width, 10000)
			)
			container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, measured.Y + bottomPadding)
		end

		updateSize()
		parent:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateSize)
		return label, container
	end

	local function closeSettings()
		if this.HubRef then
			pcall(function()
				this.HubRef:SetVisibility(false, true)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function requestScreenshot()
		closeSettings()
		task.delay(0.15, function()
			fireYekuRemoteEvent("ScreenshotEvent")
		end)
	end

	local function requestRecordingToggle()
		if recordingRequestPending then
			return
		end

		local targetState = not isRecordingVideo
		local command = targetState and "start" or "stop"
		recordingRequestPending = true
		pendingRecordingTarget = targetState
		recordingRequestGeneration += 1
		local requestGeneration = recordingRequestGeneration

		-- Do not optimistically change the caption. The executor bridge's
		-- RecordingStart/RecordingStop/RecordingEnd events are authoritative.
		local sent = fireRecordSomething(command)
		if not sent then
			recordingRequestPending = false
			pendingRecordingTarget = nil
			recordingRequestGeneration += 1
			return
		end

		closeSettings()
		task.delay(3, function()
			if recordingRequestPending and recordingRequestGeneration == requestGeneration then
				recordingRequestPending = false
				pendingRecordingTarget = nil
				warn("[Record] No matching recording status event arrived for " .. command
					.. "; the button remains in the last confirmed state.")
			end
		end)
	end

	makeTextLabel("ScreenshotTitle", "Screenshot", true, this.Page, 1)
	makeTextLabel(
		"ScreenshotBody",
		"By clicking the 'Take Screenshot' button, the menu will close and take a screenshot and save it to your computer.",
		false,
		this.Page,
		2
	)

	this.ScreenshotButtonRow, this.ScreenshotButton = utility:AddButtonRow(
		this,
		"ScreenshotButton",
		"Take Screenshot",
		UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 44),
		requestScreenshot
	)
	this.ScreenshotButtonRow.LayoutOrder = 3

	makeTextLabel("VideoTitle", "Video", true, this.Page, 4)
	makeTextLabel(
		"VideoBody",
		"By clicking the 'Record Video' button, the menu will close and start recording your screen. Click 'Stop Recording' to stop it.",
		false,
		this.Page,
		5
	)

	local recordRow
	recordRow, recordButton = utility:AddButtonRow(
		this,
		"RecordButton",
		"Record Video",
		UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 44),
		requestRecordingToggle
	)
	recordRow.LayoutOrder = 6
	updateRecordButtonText()

	local function attachEvent(eventObject, kind)
		if not eventObject or recordingStateConnections[eventObject] then
			return
		end
		local signal
		if eventObject:IsA("BindableEvent") then
			signal = eventObject.Event
		elseif eventObject:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			signal = eventObject.OnClientEvent
		else
			return
		end

		if kind == "RecordingStart" then
			recordingStateConnections[eventObject] = signal:Connect(function()
				setRecordingState(true)
			end)
		elseif kind == "RecordingStop" or kind == "RecordingEnd" then
			recordingStateConnections[eventObject] = signal:Connect(function()
				setRecordingState(false)
			end)
		elseif kind == "OnGoingRecording" then
			recordingStateConnections[eventObject] = signal:Connect(function(active)
				setRecordingState(active ~= false)
			end)
		elseif kind == "RecordingChanged" then
			recordingStateConnections[eventObject] = signal:Connect(function(active)
				if type(active) == "boolean" then
					setRecordingState(active)
				end
			end)
		elseif kind == "RecordingContinue" or kind == "RecordingResume" then
			recordingStateConnections[eventObject] = signal:Connect(function(active)
				setRecordingState(active ~= false)
			end)
		elseif kind == "RecordingPause" then
			recordingStateConnections[eventObject] = signal:Connect(function()
				setRecordingState(false)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function attachAvailableEvents()
		for _, pair in ipairs({
			{ "RecordingStart", "RecordingStart" },
			{ "RecordingStop", "RecordingStop" },
			{ "RecordingEnd", "RecordingEnd" },
			{ "OnGoingRecording", "OnGoingRecording" },
			{ "RecordingContinue", "RecordingContinue" },
			{ "RecordingResume", "RecordingResume" },
			{ "RecordingPause", "RecordingPause" },
			{ "RecordingChanged", "RecordingChanged" },
			{ "recordingstart", "RecordingStart" },
			{ "recordingstop", "RecordingStop" },
			{ "recordingend", "RecordingEnd" },
			{ "ongoingrecording", "OnGoingRecording" },
			}) do
			attachEvent(findRecordingEvent(pair[1]), pair[2])
		end
	end

	attachAvailableEvents()
	ReplicatedStorage.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		local name = string.lower(child.Name)
		if name == "recordingstart" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingStart")
		elseif name == "recordingstop" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingStop")
		elseif name == "recordingend" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingEnd")
		elseif name == "ongoingrecording" then
			attachEvent(child, "OnGoingRecording")
		elseif name == "recordingcontinue" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingContinue")
		elseif name == "recordingresume" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingResume")
		elseif name == "recordingpause" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingPause")
		elseif name == "recordingchanged" then
			attachEvent(child, "RecordingChanged")
		end
	end)

	this.Page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 400)
	return this
end

local PageInstance = Initialize()

PageInstance.Displayed.Event:Connect(function(switchedFromGamepadInput)
	if switchedFromGamepadInput then
		GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = PageInstance.ScreenshotButton
	end
end)

return PageInstance

                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: ReportAbuseMenu.lua
    Written by: jeditkacheff
    Version 1.0
    Yeku Legacy CoreGui port.

    Preserves the original Report Abuse page and its public ReportPlayer API.

-------------- SERVICES --------------
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local NewRobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local Modules = NewRobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules")
local SettingsModules = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings")

----------- UTILITIES --------------
local utility = require(SettingsModules:WaitForChild("Utility"))
local settingsPageFactory =
	require(SettingsModules:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory"))

------------ CONSTANTS --------------
local ABUSE_TYPES_PLAYER = {
	"Swearing",
	"Inappropriate Username",
	"Bullying",
	"Scamming",
	"Dating",
	"Cheating/Exploiting",
	"Personal Question",
	"Offsite Links",
}

local ABUSE_TYPES_GAME = {
	"Inappropriate Content",
	"Bad Model or Script",
	"Offsite Link",
}

local DEFAULT_ABUSE_DESC_TEXT = "   Short Description (Optional)"

if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
	DEFAULT_ABUSE_DESC_TEXT = "   (Optional)"
end

------------ VARIABLES --------------
local PageInstance = nil

----------- CLASS DECLARATION --------------

local function Initialize()
	local this = settingsPageFactory:CreateNewPage()

	local playerNames = {}
	local nameToRbxPlayer = {}

	function this:GetPlayerFromIndex(index)
		local playerName = playerNames[index]

		if playerName then
			return nameToRbxPlayer[playerName]
		end

		return nil
	end

	function this:UpdatePlayerDropDown()
		playerNames = {}
		nameToRbxPlayer = {}

		local players = PlayersService:GetPlayers()
		local index = 1

		for i = 1, #players do
			local player = players[i]

			if player ~= PlayersService.LocalPlayer and player.UserId &gt; 0 then
				playerNames[index] = player.Name
				nameToRbxPlayer[player.Name] = player
				index = index + 1
			end
		end

		this.WhichPlayerMode:UpdateDropDownList(playerNames)

		if index == 1 then
			this.GameOrPlayerMode:SetSelectionIndex(1)
			this.TypeOfAbuseMode:UpdateDropDownList(ABUSE_TYPES_GAME)
		end

		this.WhichPlayerMode:SetInteractable(
			index &gt; 1 and this.GameOrPlayerMode.CurrentIndex ~= 1
		)

		this.GameOrPlayerMode:SetInteractable(index &gt; 1)
	end

	------ TAB CUSTOMIZATION -------
	this.TabHeader.Name = "ReportAbuseTab"

	this.TabHeader.Icon.Image =
		"rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/ReportAbuseTab.png"

	if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 27, 0, 32)
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 1, 0)
	else
		this.TabHeader.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 1, 0)
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 43)
	end

	this.TabHeader.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Position.X.Scale,
		this.TabHeader.Icon.Position.X.Offset + 10,
		0.5,
		-this.TabHeader.Icon.Size.Y.Offset / 2
	)

	this.TabHeader.Icon.Title.Text = "Report"

	------ PAGE CUSTOMIZATION -------
	this.Page.Name = "ReportAbusePage"

	-- Dropdowns need a hub reference when they are initialized.
	function this:SetHub(newHubRef)
		this.HubRef = newHubRef

		if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
			this.GameOrPlayerFrame,
				this.GameOrPlayerLabel,
				this.GameOrPlayerMode = utility:AddNewRow(
					this,
					"Game or Player?",
					"Selector",
					{ "Game", "Player" },
					1
				)
		else
			this.GameOrPlayerFrame,
				this.GameOrPlayerLabel,
				this.GameOrPlayerMode = utility:AddNewRow(
					this,
					"Game or Player?",
					"Selector",
					{ "Game", "Player" },
					1,
					3
				)
		end

		this.WhichPlayerFrame,
			this.WhichPlayerLabel,
			this.WhichPlayerMode = utility:AddNewRow(
				this,
				"Which Player?",
				"DropDown",
				{ "update me" }
			)

		this.WhichPlayerMode:SetInteractable(false)
		this.WhichPlayerLabel.ZIndex = 1

		this.TypeOfAbuseFrame,
			this.TypeOfAbuseLabel,
			this.TypeOfAbuseMode = utility:AddNewRow(
				this,
				"Type Of Abuse",
				"DropDown",
				ABUSE_TYPES_GAME
			)

		if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
			this.AbuseDescriptionFrame,
				this.AbuseDescriptionLabel,
				this.AbuseDescription = utility:AddNewRow(
					this,
					DEFAULT_ABUSE_DESC_TEXT,
					"TextBox",
					nil,
					nil
				)
		else
			this.AbuseDescriptionFrame,
				this.AbuseDescriptionLabel,
				this.AbuseDescription = utility:AddNewRow(
					this,
					DEFAULT_ABUSE_DESC_TEXT,
					"TextBox",
					nil,
					nil,
					5
				)
		end

		if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
			this.AbuseDescription.Selection.Size =
				UDim2.new(0, 290, 0, 30)

			this.AbuseDescription.Selection.Position = UDim2.new(
				1,
				-345,
				this.AbuseDescription.Selection.Position.Y.Scale,
				this.AbuseDescription.Selection.Position.Y.Offset
			)

			this.AbuseDescriptionLabel = this.TypeOfAbuseLabel:Clone()
			this.AbuseDescriptionLabel.Text = "Abuse Description"

			this.AbuseDescriptionLabel.Position = UDim2.new(
				this.AbuseDescriptionLabel.Position.X.Scale,
				this.AbuseDescriptionLabel.Position.X.Offset,
				0,
				50
			)

			this.AbuseDescriptionLabel.Parent = this.Page
		end

		local SelectionOverrideObject = utility:Create "ImageLabel" {
			Image = "",
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		}

		local submitButton, submitText = nil, nil
		local playerSelectedFromContextMenu = false

		local function makeSubmitButtonActive()
			submitButton:SetAttribute("YekuKeepBlue", true)
			submitButton.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButtonSelected.png"
			submitButton.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			submitButton.ZIndex = 2
			submitButton.Selectable = true
			submitText.ZIndex = 2
		end

		this.EnsureReportCanSubmit = function()
			playerSelectedFromContextMenu = true
			if submitButton and submitButton.Parent then
				makeSubmitButtonActive()
			end
		end

		local function makeSubmitButtonInactive()
			if playerSelectedFromContextMenu then
				makeSubmitButtonActive()
				return
			end
			submitButton:SetAttribute("YekuKeepBlue", false)
			submitButton.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarAssets/MenuButton.png"
			submitButton.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			submitButton.ZIndex = 1
			submitButton.Selectable = false
			submitText.ZIndex = 1
		end

		local function updateAbuseDropDown()
			this.WhichPlayerMode:ResetSelectionIndex()
			this.TypeOfAbuseMode:ResetSelectionIndex()

			if this.GameOrPlayerMode.CurrentIndex == 1 then
				this.TypeOfAbuseMode:UpdateDropDownList(ABUSE_TYPES_GAME)
				this.WhichPlayerMode:SetInteractable(false)
				this.WhichPlayerLabel.ZIndex = 1

				this.GameOrPlayerMode.SelectorFrame.NextSelectionDown =
					this.TypeOfAbuseMode.DropDownFrame
			else
				this.TypeOfAbuseMode:UpdateDropDownList(ABUSE_TYPES_PLAYER)
				this.WhichPlayerMode:SetInteractable(true)
				this.WhichPlayerLabel.ZIndex = 2

				this.GameOrPlayerMode.SelectorFrame.NextSelectionDown =
					this.WhichPlayerMode.DropDownFrame
			end

			makeSubmitButtonInactive()
		end

		local function cleanupReportAbuseMenu()
			playerSelectedFromContextMenu = false
			updateAbuseDropDown()
			this.AbuseDescription.Selection.Text = DEFAULT_ABUSE_DESC_TEXT

			if this.HubRef then
				this.HubRef:SetVisibility(false, true)
			end
		end

		local function onReportSubmitted()
			local abuseReason = nil
			local description = this.AbuseDescription.Selection.Text

			if this.GameOrPlayerMode.CurrentIndex == 2 then
				abuseReason =
					ABUSE_TYPES_PLAYER[this.TypeOfAbuseMode.CurrentIndex]

				local currentAbusingPlayer =
					this:GetPlayerFromIndex(this.WhichPlayerMode.CurrentIndex)

				if currentAbusingPlayer and abuseReason then
					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(function()
							game.Players:ReportAbuse(
								currentAbusingPlayer,
								abuseReason,
								description
							)
						end)
					end)
				end
			else
				abuseReason =
					ABUSE_TYPES_GAME[this.TypeOfAbuseMode.CurrentIndex]

				if abuseReason then
					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(function()
							game.Players:ReportAbuse(
								nil,
								abuseReason,
								description
							)
						end)
					end)
				end
			end

			if abuseReason then
				local alertText =
					"Thanks for your report! Our moderators will review "
					.. "the chat logs and evaluate what happened."

				if abuseReason == "Cheating/Exploiting" then
					alertText =
						"Thanks for your report! We've recorded your report "
						.. "for evaluation."
				elseif abuseReason == "Inappropriate Username" then
					alertText =
						"Thanks for your report! Our moderators will evaluate "
						.. "the username."
				elseif abuseReason == "Bad Model or Script"
					or abuseReason == "Inappropriate Content"
					or abuseReason == "Offsite Link"
					or abuseReason == "Offsite Links" then
					alertText =
						"Thanks for your report! Our moderators will review "
						.. "the place and make a determination."
				end

				utility:ShowAlert(
					alertText,
					"Ok",
					this.HubRef,
					cleanupReportAbuseMenu
				)

				this.LastSelectedObject = nil
			end
		end

		submitButton, submitText = utility:MakeStyledButton(
			"SubmitButton",
			"Submit",
			UDim2.new(0, 198, 0, 50),
			onReportSubmitted,
			this
		)

		if utility:IsSmallTouchScreen() then
			submitButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -220, 1, 5)
		else
			submitButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -194, 1, 5)
		end

		submitButton.Selectable = false
		submitButton.ZIndex = 1
		submitText.ZIndex = 1

		-- Keep the original working parent: this button belongs to the
		-- custom Selection object created by the legacy TextBox utility.
		submitButton.Parent = this.AbuseDescription.Selection

		local function playerSelectionChanged(newIndex)
			if newIndex ~= nil
				and this.TypeOfAbuseMode:GetSelectedIndex() ~= nil then
				makeSubmitButtonActive()
			else
				makeSubmitButtonInactive()
			end
		end

		this.WhichPlayerMode.IndexChanged:connect(playerSelectionChanged)

		local function typeOfAbuseChanged(newIndex)
			if newIndex ~= nil then
				if this.GameOrPlayerMode.CurrentIndex == 1
					or this.WhichPlayerMode:GetSelectedIndex() ~= nil then
					makeSubmitButtonActive()
				else
					makeSubmitButtonInactive()
				end
			else
				makeSubmitButtonInactive()
			end
		end

		this.TypeOfAbuseMode.IndexChanged:connect(typeOfAbuseChanged)
		this.GameOrPlayerMode.IndexChanged:connect(updateAbuseDropDown)

		this:AddRow(nil, nil, this.AbuseDescription)

		this.Page.Size = UDim2.new(
			1,
			0,
			0,
			submitButton.AbsolutePosition.Y
				+ submitButton.AbsoluteSize.Y
		)
	end

	return this
end

----------- PUBLIC FACING API --------------

do
	PageInstance = Initialize()

	PageInstance.Displayed.Event:Connect(function()
		PageInstance:UpdatePlayerDropDown()
	end)

	-- Public API used by the player context menu and other legacy callers.
	function PageInstance:ReportPlayer(player)
		if not player or not PageInstance then return false end
		playerSelectedFromContextMenu = false
		local okPlayer, isPlayer = pcall(function() return player:IsA("Player") end)
		if not okPlayer or not isPlayer then return false end

		-- The SettingsHub can finish constructing pages after the context-menu
		-- click. Wait briefly for the real hub reference instead of failing on
		-- the first attempt (the old loader race that made this intermittent).
		local hubDeadline = os.clock() + 8
		while not PageInstance.HubRef and os.clock() &lt; hubDeadline do
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		local hub = PageInstance.HubRef
		if not hub or type(hub.SetVisibility) ~= "function" then
			warn("[ReportAbuseMenu] SettingsHub reference did not become available.")
			return false
		end

		local selected = false
		local selectionRunning = false
		local displayedConnection
		local function selectReportedPlayer()
			if selected or selectionRunning then return end
			selectionRunning = true
			task.spawn(function()
				local deadline = os.clock() + 5
				while os.clock() &lt; deadline and player.Parent == PlayersService do
					local ready = PageInstance.GameOrPlayerMode
						and PageInstance.TypeOfAbuseMode and PageInstance.WhichPlayerMode
					if ready then
						pcall(function() PageInstance:UpdatePlayerDropDown() end)
						pcall(function() PageInstance.GameOrPlayerMode:SetSelectionIndex(2) end)
						pcall(function() PageInstance.TypeOfAbuseMode:SetSelectionIndex(1) end)
						local chosen = false
						local setOK = pcall(function()
							chosen = PageInstance.WhichPlayerMode:SetSelectionByValue(player.Name) == true
						end)
						if setOK and chosen then
							task.wait(0.05)
							local index = PageInstance.WhichPlayerMode:GetSelectedIndex()
							local selectedPlayer = index and PageInstance:GetPlayerFromIndex(index)
							if selectedPlayer and selectedPlayer.UserId == player.UserId then
								selected = true
								pcall(function() PageInstance:EnsureReportCanSubmit() end)
								selectionRunning = false
								if displayedConnection then displayedConnection:Disconnect(); displayedConnection = nil end
								return
							end
						end
						end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
				selectionRunning = false
				if displayedConnection then displayedConnection:Disconnect(); displayedConnection = nil end
				warn("[ReportAbuseMenu] Could not select the requested player in the Report page: " .. tostring(player.Name))
			end)
		end

		displayedConnection = PageInstance.Displayed.Event:Connect(selectReportedPlayer)
		local openOK, openError = pcall(function()
			hub:SetVisibility(true, false, PageInstance)
		end)
		if not openOK then
			if displayedConnection then displayedConnection:Disconnect(); displayedConnection = nil end
			warn("[ReportAbuseMenu] Failed to open Report Abuse page: " .. tostring(openError))
			return false
		end
		-- Some SettingsHub versions do not fire Displayed when the page is
		-- already selected, so always schedule a second bounded attempt.
		task.delay(0.3, selectReportedPlayer)
		return true
	end
end

return PageInstance</ProtectedString>
                                        <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-148</int64>
                                        <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                    </Properties>
                                </Item>
                                <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX184">
                                    <Properties>
                                        <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                        <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                        <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                        <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                            <null />
                                        </ContentId>
                                        <string name="Name">GameSettings</string>
                                        <string name="ScriptGuid">{833d0414-df73-42c0-b2b2-e381b96e9bbb}</string>
                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: GameSettings.lua

    Preserves the SettingsPageFactory page contract and Utility API.

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local node = script
	while node and node ~= game do
		if node.Name == "NewRobloxGui" then return node end
		node = node.Parent
	end
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui") or CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui")
end
local RobloxGui = resolveRobloxGui()
local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules")
local SettingsModules = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings")
local utility = require(SettingsModules:WaitForChild("Utility"))
local settingsPageFactory = require(SettingsModules:WaitForChild("SettingsPageFactory"))

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local VRService = game:GetService("VRService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local GameSettings = UserSettings().GameSettings
local platform = UserInputService:GetPlatform()

local function getFlag(name, fallback)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return settings():GetFFlag(name) end)
	if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return fallback == true
end
local function safeRequire(parent, name)
	local module = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not module or not module:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
	local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
	return ok and value or nil
end
local TenFootInterface = safeRequire(Modules, "TenFootInterface")
local isTenFootInterface = false
if TenFootInterface and type(TenFootInterface.IsEnabled) == "function" then
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return TenFootInterface:IsEnabled() end)
	isTenFootInterface = ok and value == true
end
local useNotificationsLocalization = getFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization", false)
local advancedMouseSensitivityEnabled = getFlag("AdvancedMouseSensitivityEnabled", false)
local enableNewDevConsole = getFlag("EnableNewDevConsole", false)
local isMobile = platform == Enum.Platform.IOS or platform == Enum.Platform.Android
local isDesktop = platform == Enum.Platform.Windows or platform == Enum.Platform.OSX or platform == Enum.Platform.UWP
local useMicroProfiler = isMobile and getFlag("EnableMobileMicroProfilerWebServerApi", false)
	or isDesktop and getFlag("EnableDesktopMicroProfilerApi", false)
local enableWebServerOnStart = getFlag("EnableWebServerOnStart", false)

local GRAPHICS_QUALITY_LEVELS = 10
local function safeSet(object, property, value)
	return pcall(function() object[property] = value end)
end
local function addRow(page, name, kind, values, default, extraSpacing)
	local ok, row, label, control = pcall(function()
		return utility:AddNewRow(page, name, kind, values, default, extraSpacing)
	end)
	if ok then return row, label, control end
	warn("[Settings.GameSettings] Could not create row '" .. name .. "': " .. tostring(row))
	return nil, nil, nil
end
local function makeOverrideLabel(parent, name, text)
	return utility:Create("TextLabel") {
		Name = name, Text = text or "Set by Developer", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1),
		Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 20, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Size = UDim2.new(0.6,0,1,0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5),
		Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), Visible = false, ZIndex = 2, Parent = parent,
		TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
	}
end
local function connectSignal(signal, callback)
	if signal then
		local ok, connection = pcall(function() return signal:Connect(callback) end)
		if ok then return connection end
	end
	return nil
end
local function connectNamedSignal(object, name, callback)
	local ok, signal = pcall(function() return object[name] end)
	if ok then return connectSignal(signal, callback) end
	return nil
end

local this = settingsPageFactory:CreateNewPage()
this.Page.Name = "GameSettings"
local settingsDisabledInVR = {}
local createdRows = false

local function refreshVRVisibility()
	local vrEnabled = VRService.VREnabled
	for frame in pairs(settingsDisabledInVR) do
		if frame and frame.Parent then frame.Visible = not vrEnabled end
	end
end
connectSignal(VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"), refreshVRVisibility)

-- Fullscreen

if fsOk and not fullScreen then fullscreenDefault = 2 end

	this.FullscreenFrame.LayoutOrder = 6
	settingsDisabledInVR[this.FullscreenFrame] = true
end

	this.FullscreenEnabler.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index)
		local ok, current = pcall(function() return GameSettings:InFullScreen() end)
		if not ok then return end
		if (index == 1 and not current) or (index == 2 and current) then
			pcall(function() GuiService:ToggleFullscreen() end)
		end
	end)
end
connectNamedSignal(GameSettings, "FullscreenChanged", function(value)
	if this.FullscreenEnabler then this.FullscreenEnabler:SetSelectionIndex(value and 1 or 2) end
end)

-- Graphics quality: retain Automatic/Manual and the 1–10 selector.
local qualityEnum = Enum.SavedQualitySetting
local isAutomatic = true
pcall(function() isAutomatic = GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel == qualityEnum.Automatic end)
this.GraphicsEnablerFrame, this.GraphicsEnablerLabel, this.GraphicsQualityEnabler = addRow(this, "Graphics Mode", "Selector", {"Automatic", "Manual"}, isAutomatic and 1 or 2)
if this.GraphicsEnablerFrame then this.GraphicsEnablerFrame.LayoutOrder = 7 end
local initialQuality = 5
pcall(function()
	local saved = GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel
	if saved ~= qualityEnum.Automatic then
		local name = tostring(saved)
		local match = string.match(name, "QualityLevel(%d+)")
		initialQuality = math.clamp(tonumber(match) or 10, 1, GRAPHICS_QUALITY_LEVELS)
	end
end)
this.GraphicsQualityFrame, this.GraphicsQualityLabel, this.GraphicsQualitySlider = addRow(this, "Graphics Quality", "Slider", GRAPHICS_QUALITY_LEVELS, initialQuality)
if this.GraphicsQualityFrame then this.GraphicsQualityFrame.LayoutOrder = 8 end
if this.GraphicsQualitySlider then this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetMinStep(1) end

local function setGraphicsQuality(value, allowAutomatic)
	if not this.GraphicsQualitySlider then return end
	local numeric = tonumber(value)
	if not numeric then return end
	if numeric &lt;= 0 and not allowAutomatic then numeric = 1 end
	if numeric &gt; 0 then numeric = math.clamp(math.floor(numeric + 0.5), 1, GRAPHICS_QUALITY_LEVELS) end
	local ok = pcall(function()
		if allowAutomatic and value == 0 then
			GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel = qualityEnum.Automatic
		else
			local maxQuality = settings().Rendering:GetMaxQualityLevel()
			local qualityLevel = math.clamp(math.floor((maxQuality - 1) * numeric / GRAPHICS_QUALITY_LEVELS), 1, maxQuality - 1)
			GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel = qualityEnum["QualityLevel" .. tostring(numeric)] or numeric
			settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = qualityLevel
		end
	end)
	if not ok then
		-- On clients that do not expose legacy graphics settings, keep the UI responsive.
	end
end
local function setGraphicsAutomatic()
	if this.GraphicsQualitySlider then
		this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetZIndex(1)
		this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetInteractable(false)
	end
	if this.GraphicsQualityLabel then this.GraphicsQualityLabel.ZIndex = 1 end
	pcall(function() GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel = qualityEnum.Automatic end)
end
local function setGraphicsManual(value)
	if this.GraphicsQualitySlider then
		this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetZIndex(2)
		this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetInteractable(true)
		if this.GraphicsQualitySlider:GetValue() == value then setGraphicsQuality(value) else this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetValue(value) end
	end
	if this.GraphicsQualityLabel then this.GraphicsQualityLabel.ZIndex = 2 end
end
if this.GraphicsQualitySlider then
	this.GraphicsQualitySlider.ValueChanged:Connect(function(value) setGraphicsQuality(value, false) end)
end
if this.GraphicsQualityEnabler then
	this.GraphicsQualityEnabler.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index)
		if index == 1 then setGraphicsAutomatic() else setGraphicsManual(this.GraphicsQualitySlider and this.GraphicsQualitySlider:GetValue() or initialQuality) end
	end)
end
pcall(function()
	local request = game:FindFirstChild("GraphicsQualityChangeRequest")
	if request and request:IsA("BindableEvent") and this.GraphicsQualitySlider then
		request.Event:Connect(function(increase)
			if GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel == qualityEnum.Automatic then return end
			local value = this.GraphicsQualitySlider:GetValue() + (increase and 1 or -1)
			this.GraphicsQualitySlider:SetValue(math.clamp(value, 1, GRAPHICS_QUALITY_LEVELS))
		end)
	end
end)
if isAutomatic then setGraphicsAutomatic() else setGraphicsManual(initialQuality) end

this.PerformanceStatsFrame, this.PerformanceStatsLabel, this.PerformanceStatsMode = addRow(this, "Performance Stats", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, performanceDefault)
if this.PerformanceStatsFrame then this.PerformanceStatsFrame.LayoutOrder = 9 end
if this.PerformanceStatsMode then
	this.PerformanceStatsMode.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index)
		pcall(function() GameSettings.PerformanceStatsVisible = index == 1 end)
	end)
end

this.VolumeFrame, this.VolumeLabel, this.VolumeSlider = addRow(this, "Volume", "Slider", 10, volumeDefault)

pcall(function()
	local sounds = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Sounds")

		volumeSound = Instance.new("Sound")
		volumeSound.Name = "VolumeChangeSound"
		volumeSound.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/uuhhh.mp3"
		volumeSound.Volume = 0.5
		volumeSound.Parent = sounds
	end
end)
if this.VolumeSlider then
	this.VolumeSlider.ValueChanged:Connect(function(value)
		local volume = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 0, 0, 10) / 10
		pcall(function() GameSettings.MasterVolume = volume end)

end

-- Camera inversion is optional on clients that expose the setting.

if invertOK and invertValue ~= nil then
	this.CameraInvertedFrame, this.CameraInvertedLabel, this.CameraInvertedSelector = addRow(this, "Camera Inverted", "Selector", {"Off", "On"}, invertValue and 2 or 1)
	if this.CameraInvertedFrame then this.CameraInvertedFrame.LayoutOrder = 11; settingsDisabledInVR[this.CameraInvertedFrame] = true end

end

-- Mouse sensitivity. Advanced settings are optional, so fallback to the basic slider.
local function sensitivityToSlider(value)
	value = tonumber(value) or 1
	if value &lt;= 1 then return math.clamp(math.floor((value - 0.2) / 0.16 + 0.5), 0, 5) end
	return math.clamp(math.floor((value + 2) / 0.6 + 0.5), 5, 10)
end
local function sliderToSensitivity(value)
	value = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 5, 0, 10)
	return math.max(0.2, value &lt;= 5 and (0.16 * value + 0.2) or (0.6 * value - 2))
end

local function makeSettings2016SensitivitySlider(row, property, initialValue)
	local legacy = {}
	local stepCount = 10
	local minStep = 0
	local currentStep = sensitivityToSlider(initialValue)
	local interactable = true
	local dragging = false
	local dragInputType = nil
	local valueChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	valueChangedEvent.Name = "Settings2016CameraSensitivityChanged"
	valueChangedEvent.Parent = row
	legacy.ValueChanged = valueChangedEvent.Event
	legacy.Steps = {}

	local mouseSensitivity = property == "MouseSensitivity"
	local holderWidth = mouseSensitivity and 368 or 430
	local trackStart = mouseSensitivity and 48 or 60
	local trackEnd = mouseSensitivity and 300 or 386

	local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
	holder.Name = "Slider"
	holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
	holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, holderWidth, 0, 50)
	-- Leave the 50px numeric value box at the right edge clear of the slider.
	holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, mouseSensitivity and -428 or -494, 0.5, -25)
	holder.Active = true
	holder.ZIndex = 4
	holder.Parent = row
	legacy.SliderFrame = holder

	local function createArrow(name, image, position, anchorPoint)
		local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
		button.Name = name
		button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		button.BorderSizePixel = 0
		button.AutoButtonColor = false
		button.Image = ""
		button.AnchorPoint = anchorPoint or Vector2.new(0, 0)
		button.Position = position
		button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 50)
		button.ZIndex = 5
		button.Parent = holder

		local artwork = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		artwork.Name = name .. "Image"
		artwork.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		artwork.Image = image
		artwork.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
		artwork.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		artwork.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		artwork.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
		artwork.ZIndex = 6
		artwork.Parent = button
		return button
	end

	local leftButton = createArrow("LeftButton", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/Less.png",
		UDim2.new(0, trackStart, 0.5, 0), Vector2.new(1, 0.5))
	local rightButton = createArrow("RightButton", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/More.png",
		UDim2.new(0, trackEnd + 2, 0.5, 0), Vector2.new(0, 0.5))

	local selectedLeft = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/SelectedBarLeft.png"
	local selectedRight = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/SelectedBarRight.png"
	local unselectedLeft = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/BarLeft.png"
	local unselectedRight = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/BarRight.png"
	local blue = Color3.fromRGB(0, 162, 255)
	local grey = Color3.fromRGB(78, 84, 96)
	local gap = 4
	local barWidth = (trackEnd - trackStart - gap * (stepCount - 1)) / stepCount

	for index = 1, stepCount do
		local segment = Instance.new("ImageButton")
		segment.Name = "Step" .. tostring(index)
		segment.BackgroundColor3 = grey
		segment.BackgroundTransparency = (index == 1 or index == stepCount) and 1 or 0.36
		segment.BorderSizePixel = 0
		segment.AutoButtonColor = false
		segment.Image = ""
		segment.ImageTransparency = 0.36
		segment.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(barWidth + 0.5), 0, 25)
		segment.Position = UDim2.new(0, math.floor(trackStart + (index - 1) * (barWidth + gap) + 0.5), 0.5, -12)
		segment.ZIndex = 5
		segment.Parent = holder
		if index == 1 or index == stepCount then
			segment.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
			segment.SliceCenter = Rect.new(3, 3, 32, 21)
		end
		legacy.Steps[index] = segment
	end

	local capture = Instance.new("TextButton")
	capture.Name = "Capture"
	capture.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	capture.BorderSizePixel = 0
	capture.Text = ""
	capture.AutoButtonColor = false
	capture.Active = true
	capture.Position = UDim2.new(0, trackStart, 0, 0)
	capture.Size = UDim2.new(0, trackEnd - trackStart, 0, 48)
	capture.ZIndex = 7
	capture.Parent = holder

	local function refresh()
		for index, segment in ipairs(legacy.Steps) do
			local selected = interactable and index &lt;= currentStep
			if index == 1 then
				segment.Image = selected and selectedLeft or unselectedLeft
				segment.ImageTransparency = 0.36
			elseif index == stepCount then
				segment.Image = selected and selectedRight or unselectedRight
				segment.ImageTransparency = 0.36
			else
				segment.BackgroundColor3 = selected and blue or grey
				segment.BackgroundTransparency = 0.36
			end
			segment.Active = interactable
		end
		leftButton.Visible = interactable and currentStep &gt; minStep
		rightButton.Visible = interactable and currentStep &lt; stepCount
		holder.Active = interactable
		capture.Active = interactable
	end

	local function setValue(value, fireChanged)
		value = tonumber(value)
		if not value then return end
		value = math.clamp(math.floor(value + 0.5), minStep, stepCount)
		if value == currentStep then
			refresh()
			return
		end
		currentStep = value
		refresh()
		if fireChanged then valueChangedEvent:Fire(currentStep) end
	end

	local function setFromX(x)
		if not interactable then return end
		local first = legacy.Steps[1]
		local last = legacy.Steps[stepCount]
		if not first or not last then return end
		local startX = first.AbsolutePosition.X
		local endX = last.AbsolutePosition.X + last.AbsoluteSize.X
		local width = endX - startX
		if width &lt;= 0 then return end
		local alpha = math.clamp((x - startX) / width, 0, 1)
		setValue(math.floor(alpha * (stepCount + 1)), true)
	end

	capture.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
			or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			dragInputType = input.UserInputType
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not dragging or not interactable then return end
		if (dragInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
			or (dragInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			setFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not dragging then return end
		if (dragInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
			or (dragInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			dragging = false
			dragInputType = nil
		end
	end)
	for index, segment in ipairs(legacy.Steps) do
		local stepIndex = index
		segment.Activated:Connect(function() setValue(stepIndex, true) end)
	end
	leftButton.Activated:Connect(function() setValue(currentStep - 1, true) end)
	rightButton.Activated:Connect(function() setValue(currentStep + 1, true) end)

	function legacy:SetValue(value) setValue(value, false) end
	function legacy:GetValue() return currentStep end
	function legacy:SetInteractable(value)
		interactable = value == true
		refresh()
	end
	function legacy:SetMinStep(value)
		value = tonumber(value)
		if value then
			minStep = math.clamp(math.floor(value), 0, stepCount)
			setValue(math.max(currentStep, minStep), false)
		end
	end
	function legacy:SetZIndex(value)
		holder.ZIndex = value
		leftButton.ZIndex = value + 1
		rightButton.ZIndex = value + 1
		for _, segment in ipairs(legacy.Steps) do segment.ZIndex = value + 1 end
		capture.ZIndex = value + 2
	end

	refresh()
	return legacy
end

local function addSensitivityRow(property, title)
	local ok, current = pcall(function() return GameSettings[property] end)
	if not ok or type(current) ~= "number" then return end
	local row, label = addRow(this, title, "Slider", 10, sensitivityToSlider(current))
	if row then row.LayoutOrder = 4 end
	if row then
		local generic = row:FindFirstChild("Slider")
		if generic then generic:Destroy() end
	end
	local slider = row and makeSettings2016SensitivitySlider(row, property, current) or nil
	if slider then
		slider.ValueChanged:Connect(function(value)
			pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = sliderToSensitivity(value) end)
		end)
	end

	local numberFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	numberFrame.Name = "CameraSensitivityNumberFrame"
	numberFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 58)
	numberFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	numberFrame.BorderSizePixel = 2
	numberFrame.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 205)
	numberFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 38)
	numberFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, -19)
	numberFrame.ZIndex = 8
	numberFrame.Parent = row
	-- Keep the historical numeric camera-sensitivity readout/edit box visible.
	numberFrame.Visible = true

	local numberBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
	numberBox.Name = "Value"
	numberBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 58)
	numberBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	numberBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
	numberBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	numberBox.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	numberBox.TextSize = 18
	numberBox.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	numberBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	numberBox.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	numberBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
	numberBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
	numberBox.ZIndex = 9
	numberBox.Parent = numberFrame
	numberBox.Visible = true

	local function formatValue(value)
		local text = string.format("%.2f", tonumber(value) or 0.2)
		return text:gsub("0+$", ""):gsub("%.$", "")
	end
	local function refreshNumber(value) numberBox.Text = formatValue(value) end
	refreshNumber(current)
	local function syncFromGameSettings()
		local readOK, latest = pcall(function() return GameSettings[property] end)
		if readOK and type(latest) == "number" then
			refreshNumber(latest)
			slider:SetValue(sensitivityToSlider(latest))
		end
	end
	if slider then slider.ValueChanged:Connect(syncFromGameSettings) end
	numberBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local value = tonumber(numberBox.Text)
		if not value then
			syncFromGameSettings()
			return
		end
		value = math.clamp(value, 0.2, 4)
		local writeOK = pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = value end)
		if writeOK then
			if slider then slider:SetValue(sensitivityToSlider(value)) end
			refreshNumber(value)
		else
			syncFromGameSettings()
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		GameSettings:GetPropertyChangedSignal(property):Connect(syncFromGameSettings)
	end)

	if property == "MouseSensitivity" then
		this.MouseSensitivityNumberFrame, this.MouseSensitivityNumber = numberFrame, numberBox
	else
		this.GamepadSensitivityNumberFrame, this.GamepadSensitivityNumber = numberFrame, numberBox
	end
	return row, label, slider
end
local isDesktopMouse = UserInputService.MouseEnabled and UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
if isDesktopMouse then
	this.MouseSensitivityFrame, this.MouseSensitivityLabel, this.MouseSensitivitySlider =
		addSensitivityRow("MouseSensitivity", "Camera Sensitivity")
elseif UserInputService.GamepadEnabled then
	this.GamepadSensitivityFrame, this.GamepadSensitivityLabel, this.GamepadSensitivitySlider =
		addSensitivityRow("GamepadCameraSensitivity", "Camera Sensitivity (Gamepad)")
else
	connectSignal(UserInputService.GamepadConnected, function()
		if not this.GamepadSensitivitySlider and not isDesktopMouse then
			this.GamepadSensitivityFrame, this.GamepadSensitivityLabel, this.GamepadSensitivitySlider =
				addSensitivityRow("GamepadCameraSensitivity", "Camera Sensitivity (Gamepad)")
		end
	end)
end
-- Camera and movement modes. These registered mode APIs are CoreScript-specific on some builds;
-- keep the row hidden rather than breaking the whole page if unavailable.
local PlayerScripts = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
if PlayerScripts then
	local function addRegisteredModeRow(title, isCamera)
		local getter = isCamera and (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "GetRegisteredTouchCameraMovementModes" or "GetRegisteredComputerCameraMovementModes")
			or (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "GetRegisteredTouchMovementModes" or "GetRegisteredComputerMovementModes")
		local ok, modes = pcall(function() return PlayerScripts[getter](PlayerScripts) end)
		if not ok or type(modes) ~= "table" or #modes == 0 then return end
		local labels, map = {}, {}
		for _, mode in ipairs(modes) do
			local display = mode.Name
			if display == "Default" then display = isCamera and (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "Default (Follow)" or "Default (Classic)") or (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "Default (Thumbstick)" or "Default (Keyboard)") end
			if display == "KeyboardMouse" then display = "Keyboard + Mouse" end
			if display == "ClickToMove" then display = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "Tap to Move" or "Click to Move" end
			if display == "DynamicThumbstick" then display = "Dynamic Thumbstick" end
			table.insert(labels, display); map[display] = mode
		end
		local property = isCamera and (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "TouchCameraMovementMode" or "ComputerCameraMovementMode")
			or (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and "TouchMovementMode" or "ComputerMovementMode")
		local current = 1
		pcall(function()
			local value = GameSettings[property].Value
			for index, mode in ipairs(modes) do if mode.Value == value then current = index; break end end
		end)
		local row, label, selector = addRow(this, title, "Selector", labels, current)
		if row then row.LayoutOrder = isCamera and 2 or 3 end
		if selector then selector.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) local mode = map[labels[index]]; if mode then pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = mode end) end end) end
		return row, label, selector
	end
	this.CameraModeFrame, this.CameraModeLabel, this.CameraMode = addRegisteredModeRow("Camera Mode", true)
	this.MovementModeFrame, this.MovementModeLabel, this.MovementMode = addRegisteredModeRow("Movement Mode", false)
	if this.CameraModeFrame then settingsDisabledInVR[this.CameraModeFrame] = true end
	if this.MovementModeFrame then settingsDisabledInVR[this.MovementModeFrame] = true end
end

-- Shift lock: only display if the setting is writable and the device has mouse/keyboard.
if UserInputService.MouseEnabled and UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and not isTenFootInterface then
	local controlOk, controlMode = pcall(function() return GameSettings.ControlMode end)
	if controlOk then
		this.ShiftLockFrame, this.ShiftLockLabel, this.ShiftLockMode = addRow(this, "Shift Lock Switch", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, controlMode == Enum.ControlMode.MouseLockSwitch and 1 or 2)
		if this.ShiftLockFrame then this.ShiftLockFrame.LayoutOrder = 1; settingsDisabledInVR[this.ShiftLockFrame] = true end
		if this.ShiftLockMode then this.ShiftLockMode.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) pcall(function() GameSettings.ControlMode = index == 1 and Enum.ControlMode.MouseLockSwitch or Enum.ControlMode.Classic end) end) end
	end
end

-- MicroProfiler setting, where supported.
if useMicroProfiler then
	local property = isMobile and "MicroProfilerWebServerEnabled" or "OnScreenProfilerEnabled"
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return GameSettings[property] end)
	if ok and enabled ~= nil then
		this.MicroProfilerFrame, this.MicroProfilerLabel, this.MicroProfilerMode = addRow(this, "Micro Profiler", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, enabled and 1 or 2)
		if this.MicroProfilerFrame then this.MicroProfilerFrame.LayoutOrder = 10 end
		if this.MicroProfilerMode then this.MicroProfilerMode.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) pcall(function() GameSettings[property] = index == 1 end) end) end
	end
end

-- VR is retained as a preference row if this client exposes the setting.
local vrOK, vrSetting = pcall(function() return GameSettings.VREnabled end)
if vrOK and vrSetting ~= nil then
	this.VREnabledFrame, this.VREnabledLabel, this.VREnabledSelector = addRow(this, "VR", "Selector", {"On", "Off"}, vrSetting and 1 or 2)
	if this.VREnabledFrame then this.VREnabledFrame.LayoutOrder = 12 end
	if this.VREnabledSelector then this.VREnabledSelector.IndexChanged:Connect(function(index) pcall(function() GameSettings.VREnabled = index == 1 end) end) end
end

-- Owner-only developer console option. Optional APIs are required lazily.
local function showDeveloperConsoleButton()
	local button, label = utility:MakeStyledButton("DevConsoleButton", "Open", UDim2.new(0,300,1,-20), function()
		local names = enableNewDevConsole and {"DevConsoleMaster"} or {"DeveloperConsoleModule"}
		local module = safeRequire(Modules, names[1]) or safeRequire(Modules, names[2])
		if module then
			pcall(function() if module.SetVisibility then module:SetVisibility(true) elseif module.ToggleVisibility then module:ToggleVisibility() end end)
		end
		local hubModule = safeRequire(SettingsModules, "SettingsHub")
		if hubModule then pcall(function() hubModule:SetVisibility(false, true) end) end
	end, this)
	if not button then return end
	if label then label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans end
	button.Position = UDim2.new(1,-400,0,12)
	local row = utility:AddNewRowObject(this, "Developer Console", button)
	if row then row.LayoutOrder = 13 end
end
if LocalPlayer then
	-- Defer owner-only UI creation until the current ModuleScript require has
	-- completed. task.spawn may begin immediately inside SettingsHub's require
	-- chain and trigger a circular SettingsHub/GameSettings dependency.
	task.defer(function()
		local canUse = false
		if game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.User then
			canUse = LocalPlayer.UserId == game.CreatorId
		elseif game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group then
			local ok, rank = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetRankInGroup(game.CreatorId) end)
			canUse = ok and rank == 255
		end
		if canUse then pcall(showDeveloperConsoleButton) end
	end)
end

-- Optional console overscan UI depends on Roact/console modules; don't make the whole page fail.
if isTenFootInterface then
	pcall(function()
		local button, label = utility:MakeStyledButton("AdjustButton", "Adjust", UDim2.new(0,300,1,-20), function()
			local shell = Modules:FindFirstChild("Shell")
			if not shell then return end
			local settingsHub = safeRequire(SettingsModules, "SettingsHub")
			local overscanModule = shell:FindFirstChild("Components")
			if settingsHub and overscanModule then settingsHub:SetVisibility(false, true) end
		end, this)
		if not button then return end
		if label then label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans end
		button.Position = UDim2.new(1,-400,0,12)
		local row = utility:AddNewRowObject(this, "Safe Zone", button)
		if row then row.LayoutOrder = 14 end
	end)
end

this.TabHeader.Name = "GameSettingsTab"
this.TabHeader.Icon.Image = isTenFootInterface and "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/GameSettingsTab@2x.png" or "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/MenuBarIcons/GameSettingsTab.png"
if useNotificationsLocalization and this.TabHeader:FindFirstChild("Title") then
	this.TabHeader.Title.Text = "Settings"
elseif this.TabHeader.Icon:FindFirstChild("Title") then
	this.TabHeader.Icon.Title.Text = "Settings"
end
this.Page.ZIndex = 5
if this.PageListLayout then this.PageListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0,0) end
createdRows = true
refreshVRVisibility()

return this
