                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: PromptCreator.lua
    Yeku compatibility implementation for the legacy PromptCreator API.
    Root selection is NewRobloxGui-first; the public CreatePrompt/IsCurrentlyPrompting
    interface and confirm/cancel callback semantics match the 2019 module.
]]

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local VRService = game:GetService("VRService")

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root
	pcall(function() root = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui") end)
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	return CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15) or CoreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
end

local RobloxGui = resolveRobloxGui()
assert(RobloxGui, "PromptCreator: CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

local function findModule(root, names)
	local node = root
	for _, name in ipairs(names) do
		node = node and node:FindFirstChild(name)
	end
	return node
end
local function safeRequire(module)
	if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
		if ok then return value end
		warn("PromptCreator: optional dependency failed: " .. tostring(value))
	end
	return nil
end

local Modules = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local TenFootInterface = safeRequire(Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("TenFootInterface"))
local VRDialogModule = safeRequire(findModule(Modules, {"VR", "Dialog"}))
local isTenFoot = false
if TenFootInterface and type(TenFootInterface.IsEnabled) == "function" then
	local ok, result = pcall(function() return TenFootInterface:IsEnabled() end)
	isTenFoot = ok and result == true
end

local IsMobile = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800, 600)
local IsPhone = IsMobile and viewport.Y &lt;= 500
local IsTablet = IsMobile and not IsPhone
local IsCurrentlyPrompting = false
local IsVRMode = VRService.VREnabled
local LastInputWasGamepad = false
local SavedCoreNavigation = false
local SavedGuiNavigation = false
local SavedAutoSelect = false
local PromptCallback = nil
local LastPromptOptions = nil
local PromptDialogVR = nil

local BUTTON_IMAGE = "rbxasset://textures/ui/VR/button.png"
local BUTTON_DOWN_IMAGE = "rbxasset://textures/ui/VR/buttonSelected.png"
local PROMPT_ZINDEX = 800
local FREEZE_THUMBSTICK2_ACTION_NAME = "YekuPromptFreezeThumbstick2"
local FREEZE_ABUTTON_ACTION_NAME = "YekuPromptFreezeAButton"
local CONTROLLER_CANCEL_ACTION_NAME = "YekuPromptCreatorCancel"
local CONTROLLER_SELECT_ACTION_NAME = "YekuPromptCreatorSelect"

local PromptDialog = Instance.new("Frame")
PromptDialog.Name = "YekuLegacyPromptDialog"
PromptDialog.Size = UDim2.new(0, 438, 0, 300)
PromptDialog.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
PromptDialog.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
PromptDialog.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 27, 30)
PromptDialog.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
PromptDialog.BorderSizePixel = 0
PromptDialog.Visible = false
PromptDialog.Active = true
PromptDialog.ZIndex = PROMPT_ZINDEX
PromptDialog.Parent = RobloxGui

local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
corner.Parent = PromptDialog

local function createLabel(name, text, size, position, textSize, bold)
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.Name = name
	label.Text = text or ""
	label.Size = size
	label.Position = position
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Font = bold and Enum.Font.SourceSansBold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
	label.TextSize = textSize
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
	label.TextWrapped = true
	label.ZIndex = PROMPT_ZINDEX + 2
	return label
end

local function clearPromptContent()
	for _, child in ipairs(PromptDialog:GetChildren()) do
		if not child:IsA("UICorner") then child:Destroy() end
	end
end

local function setButtonLook(button, down)
	button.Image = down and BUTTON_DOWN_IMAGE or BUTTON_IMAGE
end

local function createButton(name, caption, position, size, callback)
	local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	button.Name = name
	button.Size = size
	button.Position = position
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.Image = BUTTON_IMAGE
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.Modal = true
	button.Selectable = true
	button.Active = true
	button.ZIndex = PROMPT_ZINDEX + 1
	button.SelectionImageObject = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	button.SelectionImageObject.Name = "EmptySelectionImage"
	button.SelectionImageObject.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.SelectionImageObject.Image = ""

	local text = createLabel(name .. "Text", caption, UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -4), UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 2), isTenFoot and 28 or 18, true)
	text.Parent = button
	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function() setButtonLook(button, true) end)
	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function() setButtonLook(button, false) end)
	button.SelectionGained:Connect(function() setButtonLook(button, true) end)
	button.SelectionLost:Connect(function() setButtonLook(button, false) end)
	button.Activated:Connect(callback)
	return button
end

local function disableControllerMovement()
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(FREEZE_THUMBSTICK2_ACTION_NAME, function() return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end, false, Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick2)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(FREEZE_ABUTTON_ACTION_NAME, function() return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
end
local function enableControllerMovement()
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(FREEZE_THUMBSTICK2_ACTION_NAME)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(FREEZE_ABUTTON_ACTION_NAME)
end
local function enableControllerInput()
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(CONTROLLER_CANCEL_ACTION_NAME, function(_, state)
		if state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and LastPromptOptions and LastPromptOptions.CancelActive then
			-- B means cancel, never confirm.
			local callback = PromptCallback
			if callback then task.spawn(callback, false) end
			local module = moduleApiTable
			module:_finish()
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(CONTROLLER_SELECT_ACTION_NAME, function(_, state)
		if state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and not GuiService.SelectedCoreObject then
			local cancel = PromptDialog:FindFirstChild("CancelButton")
			GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = cancel or PromptDialog:FindFirstChild("ConfirmButton")
		end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
	end, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect)
end
local function disableControllerInput()
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(CONTROLLER_CANCEL_ACTION_NAME)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(CONTROLLER_SELECT_ACTION_NAME)
end

local moduleApiTable = {}

function moduleApiTable:_finish()
	if not IsCurrentlyPrompting then return end
	IsCurrentlyPrompting = false
	if PromptDialogVR then pcall(function() PromptDialogVR:Close() end) end
	PromptDialog.Visible = false
	GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
	GuiService.CoreGuiNavigationEnabled = SavedCoreNavigation
	GuiService.GuiNavigationEnabled = SavedGuiNavigation
	GuiService.AutoSelectGuiEnabled = SavedAutoSelect
	enableControllerMovement()
	disableControllerInput()
end

local function finishPrompt(confirmed)
	if not IsCurrentlyPrompting then return end
	local options = LastPromptOptions or {}
	local callback = PromptCallback
	local result = confirmed == true
	if options.CancelActive == false then result = true end
	moduleApiTable:_finish()
	if type(callback) == "function" then task.spawn(callback, result) end
end

local function buildPrompt(options)
	clearPromptContent()
	PromptDialog.Size = isTenFoot and UDim2.new(0, 900, 0, 600) or (IsPhone and UDim2.new(0.92, 0, 0.86, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 438, 0, 300))
	PromptDialog.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)

	local titleHeight = isTenFoot and 66 or 52
	local title = createLabel("WindowTitle", options.WindowTitle, UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, titleHeight), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), isTenFoot and 32 or 24, true)
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.Parent = PromptDialog

	local stripe = Instance.new("Frame")
	stripe.Name = "ColorStripe"
	stripe.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
	stripe.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, titleHeight)
	stripe.BackgroundColor3 = options.StripeColor or Color3.fromRGB(3, 184, 87)
	stripe.BorderSizePixel = 0
	stripe.ZIndex = PROMPT_ZINDEX + 1
	stripe.Parent = PromptDialog

	local textLeft = 20
	local textWidth = -40
	if options.Image then
		local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		image.Name = "Image"
		image.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 120)
		image.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, titleHeight + 17)
		image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		image.Image = options.Image
		image.ZIndex = PROMPT_ZINDEX + 1
		image.Parent = PromptDialog
		textLeft = 150
		textWidth = -165
		if type(options.FetchImageFunction) == "function" then
			task.spawn(function() pcall(options.FetchImageFunction, image) end)
		end
	end
	local mainText = createLabel("MainText", options.MainText, UDim2.new(1, textWidth, 0, 145), UDim2.new(0, textLeft, 0, titleHeight + 17), isTenFoot and 27 or 20, true)
	mainText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	mainText.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	mainText.Parent = PromptDialog

	local buttonY = 1
	local buttonHeight = isTenFoot and 70 or 42
	local buttonWidth = isTenFoot and 210 or 128
	local buttonGap = 10
	local cancelActive = options.CancelActive == true
	local confirmX = cancelActive and (PromptDialog.Size.X.Scale == 0 and (PromptDialog.Size.X.Offset / 2 - buttonWidth - buttonGap / 2) or 0.5) or 0.5
	local confirmPosition
	if cancelActive then
		confirmPosition = UDim2.new(0.5, -buttonWidth - 5, 1, -buttonHeight - 12)
	else
		confirmPosition = UDim2.new(0.5, -buttonWidth / 2, 1, -buttonHeight - 12)
	end
	local confirmButton = createButton("ConfirmButton", options.ConfirmationText, confirmPosition, UDim2.new(0, buttonWidth, 0, buttonHeight), function() finishPrompt(true) end)
	confirmButton.Parent = PromptDialog
	if cancelActive then
		local cancelButton = createButton("CancelButton", options.CancelText, UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 1, -buttonHeight - 12), UDim2.new(0, buttonWidth, 0, buttonHeight), function() finishPrompt(false) end)
		cancelButton.Parent = PromptDialog
		confirmButton.NextSelectionRight = cancelButton
		cancelButton.NextSelectionLeft = confirmButton
	end
end

function moduleApiTable:CreatePrompt(promptOptions)
	if IsCurrentlyPrompting then return false end
	assert(type(promptOptions) == "table", "CreatePrompt expects an options table")
	local options = {}
	for key, value in pairs(promptOptions) do options[key] = value end
	local defaults = {
		WindowTitle = "Confirm", MainText = "Is this okay?", ConfirmationText = "Confirm",
		CancelText = "Cancel", CancelActive = true, StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(3, 184, 87),
	}
	for key, value in pairs(defaults) do if options[key] == nil then options[key] = value end end

	IsCurrentlyPrompting = true
	PromptCallback = options.PromptCompletedCallback
	LastPromptOptions = options
	SavedCoreNavigation = GuiService.CoreGuiNavigationEnabled
	SavedGuiNavigation = GuiService.GuiNavigationEnabled
	SavedAutoSelect = GuiService.AutoSelectGuiEnabled
	GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
	GuiService.CoreGuiNavigationEnabled = true
	GuiService.GuiNavigationEnabled = false
	GuiService.AutoSelectGuiEnabled = false

	buildPrompt(options)
	PromptDialog.Visible = true
	disableControllerMovement()
	enableControllerInput()
	if VRService.VREnabled and VRDialogModule then
		if not PromptDialogVR then
			local ok, value = pcall(function() return VRDialogModule.new() end)
			if ok then PromptDialogVR = value end
		end
		if PromptDialogVR then
			pcall(function() PromptDialogVR:SetContent(PromptDialog); PromptDialogVR:Show(true) end)
		end
	end
	if LastInputWasGamepad then
		GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = PromptDialog:FindFirstChild("CancelButton") or PromptDialog:FindFirstChild("ConfirmButton")
	end
	return true
end

function moduleApiTable:IsCurrentlyPrompting()
	return IsCurrentlyPrompting
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	local t = input.UserInputType
	LastInputWasGamepad = t == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 or t == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2 or t == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3 or t == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4
end)

VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(function()
	IsVRMode = VRService.VREnabled
end)

return moduleApiTable

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: PlayerPermissionsModule.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch: safe group/rank lookups and per-user caches.
    This module does not depend on CoreGui.RobloxGui.
]]

local PlayerPermissionsModule = {}

local function makePlayerCacheLookup(callback)
	local cache = {}
	return function(player)
		if not player or typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then
			return false
		end
		local userId = player.UserId
		if type(userId) ~= "number" or userId &lt;= 0 then
			return false
		end
		local cached = cache[userId]
		if cached ~= nil then
			return cached
		end
		local ok, value = pcall(callback, player)
		value = ok and value == true or false
		cache[userId] = value
		return value
	end
end

local function HasRankInGroupFunctionFactory(groupId, requiredRank)
	assert(type(groupId) == "number", "groupId must be a number")
	assert(type(requiredRank) == "number", "requiredRank must be a number")
	return makePlayerCacheLookup(function(player)
		return player:GetRankInGroup(groupId) &gt;= requiredRank
	end)
end

local function IsInGroupFunctionFactory(groupId)
	assert(type(groupId) == "number", "groupId must be a number")
	return makePlayerCacheLookup(function(player)
		return player:IsInGroup(groupId)
	end)
end

PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerAdminAsync = IsInGroupFunctionFactory(1200769)
PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerInternAsync = HasRankInGroupFunctionFactory(2868472, 100)
PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerStarAsync = IsInGroupFunctionFactory(4199740)

return PlayerPermissionsModule

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local moduleApiTable = {}
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
while not LocalPlayer do Players.PlayerAdded:Wait(); LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer end

local function makeSignal()
    local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
    local signal = { Event = event.Event }
    function signal:fire(...) event:Fire(...) end
    function signal:Fire(...) event:Fire(...) end
    function signal:connect(callback) return event.Event:Connect(callback) end
    function signal:Connect(callback) return event.Event:Connect(callback) end
    function signal:wait() return event.Event:Wait() end
    function signal:Wait() return event.Event:Wait() end
    function signal:Destroy() event:Destroy() end
    return signal
end

moduleApiTable.FollowerStatusChanged = makeSignal()
moduleApiTable.PlayerBlockedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerUnblockedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerMutedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerUnmutedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerFriendedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerUnfriendedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

local function registerGetCore(name, value)
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:RegisterGetCore(name, function() return value end)
    end)
end
registerGetCore("PlayerBlockedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerBlockedEvent)
registerGetCore("PlayerUnblockedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerUnblockedEvent)
registerGetCore("PlayerMutedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerMutedEvent)
registerGetCore("PlayerUnmutedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerUnmutedEvent)

local BlockChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local MuteChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local BLOCKED = {}
local MUTED = {}
local blockListLoaded = false
local blockListLoading = false
local csrfToken = nil
local environments = {}
local requestFunction
local requestName

local function addEnvironment(env)
    if type(env) == "table" then
        for _, old in ipairs(environments) do if old == env then return end end
        table.insert(environments, env)
    end
end
if type(getgenv) == "function" then local ok, env = pcall(getgenv); if ok then addEnvironment(env) end end
if type(getfenv) == "function" then local ok, env = pcall(getfenv, 0); if ok then addEnvironment(env) end end
addEnvironment(_G)

local function findRequest()
    for _, env in ipairs(environments) do
        local candidates = {
            {"request", env.request}, {"http_request", env.http_request},
            {"httprequest", env.httprequest},
            {"http.request", type(env.http)=="table" and env.http.request or nil},
            {"syn.request", type(env.syn)=="table" and env.syn.request or nil},
            {"fluxus.request", type(env.fluxus)=="table" and env.fluxus.request or nil},
        }
        for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
            if type(candidate[2]) == "function" then return candidate[2], candidate[1] end
        end
    end
    return nil, nil
end
requestFunction, requestName = findRequest()

local function getCookie()
    for _, env in ipairs(environments) do
        local value = env.YekuRobloxCookie or env.ROBLOSECURITY or env.RobloxCookie
        if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
            value = value:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("^Cookie:%s*", "")
            if value:match("^%.ROBLOSECURITY=") then return value end
            return ".ROBLOSECURITY=" .. value
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function responseHeader(headers, wanted)
    if type(headers) ~= "table" then return nil end
    for key, value in pairs(headers) do
        if string.lower(tostring(key)) == string.lower(wanted) then return tostring(value) end
    end
    return nil
end

local function normalizeResponse(response)
    if type(response) == "string" then return {StatusCode=200, Body=response, Headers={}} end
    if type(response) ~= "table" then error("Executor HTTP "..tostring(requestName).." returned "..typeof(response)) end
    return {
        StatusCode=tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.statusCode or response.status),
        Body=response.Body or response.body or "",
        Headers=response.Headers or response.headers or {},
        Success=response.Success ~= nil and response.Success or response.success,
    }
end

local function executorRequest(method, url, body)
    if type(requestFunction) ~= "function" then
        error("No executor request/http_request API is available for Roblox's authenticated block API")
    end
    local headers = {Accept="application/json", ["Content-Type"]="application/json"}
    local cookie = getCookie(); if cookie then headers.Cookie = cookie end
    if csrfToken then headers["X-CSRF-TOKEN"] = csrfToken end
    local options = {Url=url, Method=method, Headers=headers, Timeout=20}
    if method ~= "GET" then options.Body = body or "" end
    local response = normalizeResponse(requestFunction(options))
    csrfToken = responseHeader(response.Headers, "x-csrf-token") or csrfToken
    local status = response.StatusCode
    if status == 403 and method ~= "GET" and csrfToken then
        headers["X-CSRF-TOKEN"] = csrfToken
        options.Headers = headers
        response = normalizeResponse(requestFunction(options))
        csrfToken = responseHeader(response.Headers, "x-csrf-token") or csrfToken
        status = response.StatusCode
    end
    if not status or status &lt; 200 or status &gt;= 300 or response.Success == false then
        error("HTTP "..tostring(status or "unknown")..": "..string.sub(tostring(response.Body),1,1200))
    end
    if type(response.Body) == "table" then return response.Body end
    if type(response.Body) == "string" and response.Body ~= "" then
        local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(response.Body) end)
        if ok then return decoded end
    end
    return {}
end

local function api(method, url, body)
    return executorRequest(method, url, body)
end

local function loadBlockedList()
    if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt;= 0 then BLOCKED = {}; blockListLoaded = true; return true end
    local fresh, cursor, page, success = {}, "", 0, false
    repeat
        page = page + 1
        local url = "https://apis.roblox.com/user-blocking-api/v1/users/get-blocked-users?cursor="
            .. HttpService:UrlEncode(cursor) .. "&amp;count=50"
        local ok, result = pcall(api, "GET", url)
        if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
            warn("[Yeku 2020 Block] Block-list request failed: "..tostring(result))
            break
        end
        success = true
        local rows = result.blockedUsers or result.blockedUserIds or result.userList or result.data
        if type(rows) == "table" then
            for _, item in pairs(rows) do
                local id = type(item)=="table" and tonumber(item.userId or item.id or item.blockeeId or item.UserId) or tonumber(item)
                if id and id &gt; 0 then fresh[id] = true end
            end
        end
        cursor = result.nextPageCursor or result.nextCursor or ""
    until cursor == "" or page &gt;= 10
    if success then
        BLOCKED = fresh
    else
        -- Last-resort engine cache only lists blocked users currently in this server;
        -- it is not treated as a replacement for the full account block-list API.
        local okIds, ids = pcall(function() return Players:GetBlockedUserIds() end)
        if okIds and type(ids) == "table" then
            for _, id in ipairs(ids) do
                id = tonumber(id)
                if id and id &gt; 0 then BLOCKED[id] = true end
            end
        end
    end
    blockListLoaded = success == true
    return success
end

local function beginBlockListLoad()
    if blockListLoading or blockListLoaded then return end
    blockListLoading = true
    task.spawn(function()
        loadBlockedList()
        blockListLoading = false
    end)
end
beginBlockListLoad()

local function waitForBlockList()
    if not blockListLoaded then
        beginBlockListLoad()
        local deadline = os.clock() + 5
        while not blockListLoaded and blockListLoading and os.clock() &lt; deadline do task.wait(0.1) end
    end
end

local function getBlockedIds()
    waitForBlockList()
    local ids = {}
    for id in pairs(BLOCKED) do table.insert(ids, id) end
    table.sort(ids)
    return ids
end

local function isBlocked(userId)
    -- Do not stall native 2020 PlayerList/context UI on an HTTP block-list fetch.
    -- beginBlockListLoad() refreshes BLOCKED asynchronously.
    return BLOCKED[tonumber(userId)] == true
end

local function blockPlayer(player, shouldBlock)
    if not player or not player:IsA("Player") or player == LocalPlayer or player.UserId &lt;= 0 then return false end
    local id = player.UserId
    if shouldBlock and BLOCKED[id] == true then return true end
    if not shouldBlock and BLOCKED[id] ~= true then return true end
    local action = shouldBlock and "block-user" or "unblock-user"
    local url = "https://apis.roblox.com/user-blocking-api/v1/users/"..tostring(id).."/"..action
    local ok, result = pcall(api, "POST", url, "")
    if not ok then
        warn("[Yeku 2020 Block] "..action.." failed for "..tostring(id)..": "..tostring(result))
        return false
    end
    BLOCKED[id] = shouldBlock and true or nil
    blockListLoaded = true
    BlockChanged:Fire(id, shouldBlock)
    if shouldBlock then moduleApiTable.PlayerBlockedEvent:Fire(player)
    else moduleApiTable.PlayerUnblockedEvent:Fire(player) end
    return true
end

local function getFriendCount(userId)
    local HttpRbxApiService
    pcall(function() HttpRbxApiService = game:GetService("HttpRbxApiService") end)
    if not HttpRbxApiService or type(HttpRbxApiService.GetAsyncFullUrl) ~= "function" then return nil end
    local ok, raw = pcall(function()
        return HttpRbxApiService:GetAsyncFullUrl(
            "https://friends.roblox.com/v1/users/"..tostring(userId).."/friends/count",
            Enum.ThrottlingPriority.Default, Enum.HttpRequestType.Players)
    end)
    if not ok then return nil end
    local decoded
    if type(raw)=="table" then decoded=raw else pcall(function() decoded=HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end) end
    return type(decoded)=="table" and tonumber(decoded.count) or nil
end

function moduleApiTable:CreatePlayerDropDown()
    -- Keep the native 2020 PlayerList/context menu; never add a duplicate popup.
    return nil
end
function moduleApiTable:GetFriendCountAsync(player)
    local id = typeof(player)=="Instance" and player.UserId or tonumber(player)
    return id and id &gt; 0 and getFriendCount(id) or nil
end
function moduleApiTable:InitBlockListAsync()
    beginBlockListLoad()
    return true
end
function moduleApiTable:MaxFriendCount() return 200 end
function moduleApiTable:GetFriendStatus(player)
    if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend end
    local ok, status = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetFriendStatus(player) end)
    return ok and status or Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown
end
function moduleApiTable:CreateBlockingUtility()
    local utility = {}
    function utility:BlockPlayerAsync(player) return blockPlayer(player, true) end
    function utility:UnblockPlayerAsync(player) return blockPlayer(player, false) end
    function utility:MutePlayer(player)
        if not player or player == LocalPlayer then return false end
        MUTED[player.UserId] = true; MuteChanged:Fire(player.UserId, true)
        moduleApiTable.PlayerMutedEvent:Fire(player); return true
    end
    function utility:UnmutePlayer(player)
        if not player then return false end
        MUTED[player.UserId] = nil; MuteChanged:Fire(player.UserId, false)
        moduleApiTable.PlayerUnmutedEvent:Fire(player); return true
    end
    function utility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(userId) return isBlocked(userId) end
    function utility:IsPlayerMutedByUserId(userId) return MUTED[tonumber(userId)] == true end
    function utility:GetBlockedStatusChangedEvent() return BlockChanged.Event end
    function utility:GetMutedStatusChangedEvent() return MuteChanged.Event end
    function utility:GetBlockedUserIdsAsync() return getBlockedIds() end
    return utility
end

return moduleApiTable

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: GameTranslator.lua
    Yeku CoreScript localization bridge.
    Keeps player/game translation behavior and bootstraps CoreScript AutoLocalize.

local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function getFlag(name, fallback)
    local ok, value = pcall(function() return settings():GetFFlag(name) end)
    if ok then return value end
    return fallback
end

-- When this FFlag is absent on older clients, keep translation enabled and let
-- the translator safely fall back to the original English text.
local CoreScriptTranslateGameText = getFlag("CoreScriptTranslateGameText2", true)

local function getRobloxTranslator()
    local modules = script.Parent
    local module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator")
    if not module then
        local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
        modules = root and root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator")
    end
    if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        if ok and type(result) == "table" then
            return result
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local CoreScriptTranslator = getRobloxTranslator()
if CoreScriptTranslator and type(CoreScriptTranslator.AttachCoreGui) == "function" then
    pcall(function() CoreScriptTranslator:AttachCoreGui() end)
end

local playerTranslator = nil
local player = nil
local localeId = nil
local localeIdConnection = nil
local translatorFetched = false
local localeChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local registryInfoMap = {}

local function handlePlayerOrLocaleChanged()
    if player then
        local currentLocale
        pcall(function() currentLocale = player.LocaleId end)
        if currentLocale and currentLocale ~= localeId then
            local hadLocale = localeId ~= nil
            localeId = currentLocale
            playerTranslator = nil
            translatorFetched = false
            if hadLocale then
                localeChangedEvent:Fire(localeId)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function reset()
    playerTranslator = nil
    player = nil
    localeId = nil
    translatorFetched = false
    if localeIdConnection then
        localeIdConnection:Disconnect()
        localeIdConnection = nil
    end
end

local function getTranslator()
    local currentPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if currentPlayer ~= player then
        reset()
        player = currentPlayer
    end
    if not player then return nil end

    handlePlayerOrLocaleChanged()
    if not localeIdConnection then
        local okSignal, signal = pcall(function()
            return player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocaleId")
        end)
        if okSignal and signal then
            localeIdConnection = signal:Connect(handlePlayerOrLocaleChanged)
        end
    end

    if not translatorFetched then
        translatorFetched = true
        -- This translator follows the player's experience-language locale.
        local ok, translator = pcall(function()
            return LocalizationService:GetTranslatorForPlayerAsync(player)
        end)
        if not ok then
            -- Compatibility path for older client builds.
            ok, translator = pcall(function()
                return LocalizationService:GetTranslatorForPlayer(player)
            end)
        end
        if ok then playerTranslator = translator end
    end
    return playerTranslator
end

local function isInsideGame(element)
    local ok, result = pcall(function() return game:IsAncestorOf(element) end)
    return ok and result or false
end

local GameTranslator = {}
GameTranslator.LocaleChanged = localeChangedEvent.Event

function GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(context, text)
    if type(text) ~= "string" or not CoreScriptTranslateGameText then
        return text
    end
    local translator = getTranslator()
    if translator then
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return translator:Translate(context, text)
        end)
        if ok and type(result) == "string" and result ~= "" then
            return result
        end
        -- Keep compatibility with CoreScript builds that expose the internal alias.
        ok, result = pcall(function()
            return translator:RobloxOnlyTranslate(context, text)
        end)
        if ok and type(result) == "string" and result ~= "" then
            return result
        end
    end
    return text
end

local function unregisterGui(element)
    local info = registryInfoMap[element]
    if info then
        if info.connection then info.connection:Disconnect() end
        registryInfoMap[element] = nil
    end
end

local function registerGui(element, context, text)
    local info = registryInfoMap[element]
    if info then
        info.context, info.text = context, text
        return
    end
    info = {context = context, text = text, hasBeenAdded = isInsideGame(element)}
    info.connection = element.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if not isInsideGame(element) then
            if info.hasBeenAdded then unregisterGui(element) end
        else
            info.hasBeenAdded = true
        end
    end)
    registryInfoMap[element] = info
end

function GameTranslator:TranslateAndRegister(element, context, text)
    if not element then return text end
    if CoreScriptTranslateGameText then
        element.Text = self:TranslateGameText(context, text)
        registerGui(element, context, text)
    else
        element.Text = text
    end
    return text
end

local function retranslateAll()
    for element, info in pairs(registryInfoMap) do
        if element and element.Parent then
            element.Text = GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(info.context, info.text)
        else
            unregisterGui(element)
        end
    end
end

localeChangedEvent.Event:Connect(function()
    retranslateAll()
end)

if CoreScriptTranslateGameText then
    pcall(function() LocalizationService.AutoTranslateWillRun:Connect(retranslateAll) end)
    Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Connect(function()
        reset()
        getTranslator()
        retranslateAll()
    end)
end

return GameTranslator

local HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR = Color3.new(27/255, 252/255, 107/255)
local DISPLAY_POS_INIT_INSET = 0
local DISPLAY_ITEM_OFFSET = 4
local FORCE_TEN_FOOT_INTERFACE = false

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local function platformIs(name)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return Enum.Platform[name] end)
	return ok and value ~= nil and UserInputService:GetPlatform() == value
end

local tenFootInterfaceEnabled = platformIs("XBoxOne") or platformIs("WiiU") or
	platformIs("PS4") or platformIs("AndroidTV") or platformIs("XBox360") or
	platformIs("PS3") or platformIs("Ouya") or platformIs("SteamOS")

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root
	pcall(function() root = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui") end)
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	return CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15) or CoreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
end

local function create(className, properties)
	local object = Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in pairs(properties or {}) do
		pcall(function() object[key] = value end)
	end
	return object
end

local function CreateModule()
	local api = {}
	local displayStack = {}
	local stackChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local healthChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

	local function createContainer()
		if not api.Container then
			api.Container = create("ImageButton", {
				Name = "TopRightContainer",
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 350, 0, 100),
				Position = UDim2.new(1, -415, 0, 10),
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				Image = "",
				Active = false,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Parent = RobloxGui,
			})
		end
	end

	local function repositionStack()
		local y = DISPLAY_POS_INIT_INSET
		for _, item in ipairs(displayStack) do
			if item and item.Parent then
				item.Position = UDim2.new(item.Position.X.Scale, item.Position.X.Offset, 0, y)
				if item.Visible then y = y + item.AbsoluteSize.Y + DISPLAY_ITEM_OFFSET end
			end
		end
		stackChanged:Fire()
	end

	local function addToDisplayStack(item)
		for i = #displayStack, 1, -1 do
			if displayStack[i] == item then table.remove(displayStack, i) end
		end
		table.insert(displayStack, item)
		createContainer()
		item.Parent = api.Container
		item:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(repositionStack)
		item:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(repositionStack)
		repositionStack()
	end

	function api:CreateHealthBar()
		self.HealthContainer = create("Frame", {
			Name = "HealthContainer", Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 50),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
		})
		local holder = create("Frame", {
			Name = "HealthFillHolder", Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = self.HealthContainer,
		})
		local fill = create("Frame", {
			Name = "HealthFill", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			BackgroundColor3 = HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = holder,
		})
		local label = create("TextLabel", {
			Name = "HealthText", Size = UDim2.new(0, 98, 0, 50),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, -100, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans,
			TextSize = 36, Text = "Health", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = self.HealthContainer,
		})
		local username = create("TextLabel", {Visible = false})
		local accountType = create("TextLabel", {Visible = false})
		addToDisplayStack(self.HealthContainer)
		createContainer()
		self.HealthContainer.Changed:Connect(function() healthChanged:Fire() end)
		return self.Container, username, self.HealthContainer, fill, accountType
	end

	function api:CreateAccountType(accountTypeTextShort)
		self.AccountTypeContainer = create("Frame", {
			Name = "AccountTypeContainer", Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -55, 0, 10), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
			Parent = RobloxGui,
		})
		create("TextLabel", {
			Name = "AccountTypeText", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 36,
			Text = tostring(accountTypeTextShort or ""), TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Parent = self.AccountTypeContainer,
		})
		return self.AccountTypeContainer
	end

	function api:SetupTopStat()
		local topStatEnabled = true
		local displayedStat = nil
		local changedConnection = nil
		local parentConnection = nil
		local childConnection = nil
		local statFrame = nil

		local function makeStatFrame()
			if statFrame then return end
			statFrame = create("Frame", {
				Name = "OneStatFrame", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			})
			create("TextLabel", {
				Name = "StatName", Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 36),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 36,
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				Parent = statFrame,
			})
			create("TextLabel", {
				Name = "StatValue", Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 36, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
				Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Parent = statFrame,
			})
			addToDisplayStack(statFrame)
		end

		local function isValidStat(obj)
			return obj and (obj:IsA("StringValue") or obj:IsA("IntValue") or obj:IsA("BoolValue") or
				obj:IsA("NumberValue") or obj:IsA("DoubleConstrainedValue") or obj:IsA("IntConstrainedValue"))
		end

		local function refreshStat()
			if not statFrame then return end
			local nameLabel = statFrame:FindFirstChild("StatName")
			local valueLabel = statFrame:FindFirstChild("StatValue")
			if displayedStat and displayedStat.Parent and topStatEnabled then
				nameLabel.Text = " " .. displayedStat.Name .. ":"
				valueLabel.Text = tostring(displayedStat.Value)
			else
				nameLabel.Text, valueLabel.Text = "", ""
			end
		end

		local function setDisplayedStat(stat)
			if changedConnection then changedConnection:Disconnect(); changedConnection = nil end
			if parentConnection then parentConnection:Disconnect(); parentConnection = nil end
			displayedStat = stat
			if displayedStat then
				makeStatFrame()
				changedConnection = displayedStat.Changed:Connect(refreshStat)
				parentConnection = displayedStat.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
					if not displayedStat.Parent then setDisplayedStat(nil) else refreshStat() end
				end)
			end
			refreshStat()
		end

		local function scanLeaderstats()
			local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
			local leaderstats = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
			if not leaderstats then return end
			if childConnection then childConnection:Disconnect() end
			for _, child in ipairs(leaderstats:GetChildren()) do
				if not displayedStat and isValidStat(child) then setDisplayedStat(child) end
			end
			childConnection = leaderstats.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if not displayedStat and isValidStat(child) then setDisplayedStat(child) end
			end)
		end

		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		if localPlayer then
			localPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child.Name == "leaderstats" then scanLeaderstats() end
			end)
			scanLeaderstats()
		end

		local result = {}
		function result:SetTopStatEnabled(value)
			topStatEnabled = value == true
			refreshStat()
		end
		return result
	end
	return api
end

local TenFootInterfaceModule = CreateModule()
local moduleApiTable = {}
function moduleApiTable:IsEnabled() return tenFootInterfaceEnabled end
function moduleApiTable:CreateHealthBar() return TenFootInterfaceModule:CreateHealthBar() end
function moduleApiTable:CreateAccountType(text) return TenFootInterfaceModule:CreateAccountType(text) end
function moduleApiTable:SetupTopStat() return TenFootInterfaceModule:SetupTopStat() end
return moduleApiTable

                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: ChatUtil.lua
    Yeku legacy CoreGui compatibility patch.

    Provides the historical Signal API used by legacy chat modules:
        signal:fire(...)
        signal:connect(callback)
        signal:wait()

    Also exposes the modern spellings:
        signal:Fire(...)
        signal:Connect(callback)
        signal:Wait()
        signal:Destroy()

    The BindableEvent receives the fired arguments directly. This avoids
    the original implementation's shared mArgData/mArgDataCount state,
    which could be overwritten when multiple fires occurred close together.
]]

local Util = {}

function Util.Signal()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	bindable.Name = "ChatUtilSignal"

	local signal = {
		_bindable = bindable,
		_destroyed = false,
	}

	local function assertAlive()
		if signal._destroyed then
			error("attempt to use a destroyed ChatUtil signal", 3)
		end
	end

	-- Historical method name used by older CoreScripts.
	function signal:fire(...)
		assertAlive()
		bindable:Fire(...)
	end

	-- Historical method name used by older CoreScripts.
	function signal:connect(callback)
		assertAlive()
		if type(callback) ~= "function" then
			error("connect(callback) expects a function", 2)
		end
		return bindable.Event:Connect(callback)
	end

	-- Yields the current thread until the next fire and returns every argument.
	function signal:wait()
		assertAlive()
		return bindable.Event:Wait()
	end

	-- Modern spellings share the same implementation and calling convention.
	signal.Fire = signal.fire
	signal.Connect = signal.connect
	signal.Wait = signal.wait

	function signal:Destroy()
		if self._destroyed then
			return
		end
		self._destroyed = true
		bindable:Destroy()
		self._bindable = nil
	end

	signal.destroy = signal.Destroy

	return signal
end

return Util

local function TryRequire(moduleScript, label)
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not ok then
		warn("[Chat] Optional module failed to load: " .. tostring(label) .. " - " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end
	return result
end

		RegisterModule = function() end,
		GetModule = function() return { VRIsExclusive = false } end,
		FireModuleOpened = function() end,
		FireModuleClosed = function() end,
	}
end

local function HasTextChatBackend()
	if ChatBackend and type(ChatBackend.ClassicChatEnabled) == "function" then
		local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
			return ChatBackend:ClassicChatEnabled()
		end)
		return ok and enabled == true
	end
	return false
end

local function IsBubbleChatOnly()
	if ChatBackend and type(ChatBackend.IsBubbleChatOnly) == "function" then
		local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
			return ChatBackend:IsBubbleChatOnly()
		end)
		return ok and enabled == true
	end
	return false
end

local DisplayChatError = nil

local function SendBackendMessage(text, channelName)
	if not ChatBackend or type(ChatBackend.SendMessage) ~= "function" then
		return false, "The NewChat TextChatService backend is missing."
	end

	local callOK, sent, result = pcall(function()
		return ChatBackend:SendMessage(text, channelName)
	end)
	if not callOK then
		return false, tostring(sent)
	end
	if sent ~= true then
		return false, tostring(result or "TextChatService rejected the message.")
	end
	return true, result
end

local function FindBackendChannelForMode(messageMode, targetPlayer)
	if not ChatBackend or type(ChatBackend.GetTextChannels) ~= "function" then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, channels = pcall(function()
		return ChatBackend:GetTextChannels()
	end)
	if not ok or type(channels) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	local targetId = targetPlayer and tostring(targetPlayer.UserId) or nil
	local targetName = targetPlayer and string.lower(targetPlayer.Name) or nil
	for _, channel in ipairs(channels) do
		if typeof(channel) == "Instance" and channel:IsA("TextChannel") then
			local name = string.lower(channel.Name)
			if messageMode == "Team" and string.find(name, "team", 1, true) then
				return channel.Name
			elseif messageMode == "Whisper" and string.find(name, "whisper", 1, true) then
				if (targetId and string.find(name, targetId, 1, true))
					or (targetName and string.find(name, targetName, 1, true)) then
					return channel.Name
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function SendThroughTextChat(text, messageMode, targetPlayer)
	if not ChatBackend or type(ChatBackend.SendMessage) ~= "function" then
		return false, "The NewChat TextChatService backend is missing."
	end

	if messageMode ~= "Team" and messageMode ~= "Whisper" then
		return SendBackendMessage(text)
	end

	if messageMode == "Whisper" and not targetPlayer then
		return false, "Invalid whisper target."
	end

	local channelName = FindBackendChannelForMode(messageMode, targetPlayer)
	if channelName then
		return SendBackendMessage(text, channelName)
	end

	-- The default TextChatCommand prepares team/whisper routing. Send the
	-- command first, then wait briefly for its TextChannel to become available;
	-- never send the actual message as a slash command (that would be consumed).
	local commandText = messageMode == "Team"
		and "/team"
		or ("/w " .. targetPlayer.Name)
	local initiated, initError = SendBackendMessage(commandText)
	if not initiated then
		return false, initError
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		for _ = 1, 30 do
			task.wait(0.1)
			local readyChannel = FindBackendChannelForMode(messageMode, targetPlayer)
			if readyChannel then
				local sent, sendError = SendBackendMessage(text, readyChannel)
				if not sent then
					if DisplayChatError then
						DisplayChatError(sendError)
					else
						warn("[Chat] Could not send routed chat message: " .. tostring(sendError))
					end
				end
				return
			end
		end

		local message = messageMode == "Team"
			and "The team TextChannel did not become available."
			or "The whisper TextChannel for " .. targetPlayer.Name .. " did not become available."
		if DisplayChatError then
			DisplayChatError(message)
		else
			warn("[Chat] " .. message)
		end
	end)

	return true
end

local Util = {}
do
	-- Check if we are running on a touch device
	function Util.IsTouchDevice()
		local touchEnabled = false
		pcall(function() touchEnabled = InputService.TouchEnabled end)
		return touchEnabled
	end

	function Util.IsSmallScreenSize()
		return GuiRoot.AbsoluteSize.X &lt;= PHONE_SCREEN_WIDTH
	end

	function Util.Create(instanceType)
		return function(data)
			local obj = Instance.new(instanceType)
			for k, v in pairs(data) do
				if type(k) == 'number' then
					v.Parent = obj
				else
					obj[k] = v
				end
			end
			return obj
		end
	end

	function Util.Clamp(low, high, input)
		return math.max(low, math.min(high, input))
	end

	function Util.Linear(t, b, c, d)
		if t &gt;= d then return b + c end

		return c*t/d + b
	end

	function Util.EaseOutQuad(t, b, c, d)
		if t &gt;= d then return b + c end

		t = t/d;
		return -c * t*(t-2) + b
	end

	function Util.EaseInOutQuad(t, b, c, d)
		if t &gt;= d then
			return b + c
		end

		t = t / (d/2);
		if (t &lt; 1) then
			return c/2*t*t + b
		end;
		t = t - 1;
		return -c/2 * (t*(t-2) - 1) + b;
	end

	function Util.PropertyTweener(instance, prop, start, final, duration, easingFunc, cbFunc)
		local this = {}
		this.StartTime = tick()
		this.EndTime = this.StartTime + duration
		this.Cancelled = false

		local finished = false
		local percentComplete = 0
		spawn(function()
			local now = tick()
			while now &lt; this.EndTime and instance do
				if this.Cancelled then
					return
				end
				instance[prop] = easingFunc(now - this.StartTime, start, final - start, duration)
				percentComplete = Util.Clamp(0, 1, (now - this.StartTime) / duration)
				RunService.RenderStepped:wait()
				now = tick()
			end
			if this.Cancelled == false and instance then
				instance[prop] = final
				finished = true
				percentComplete = 1
				if cbFunc then
					cbFunc()
				end
			end
		end)

		function this:GetPercentComplete()
			return percentComplete
		end

		function this:IsFinished()
			return finished
		end

		function this:Cancel()
			this.Cancelled = true
		end

		return this
	end

	function Util.Signal()
		local sig = {}

		local mSignaler = Instance.new('BindableEvent')

		local mArgData = nil
		local mArgDataCount = nil

		function sig:fire(...)
			mArgData = {...}
			mArgDataCount = select('#', ...)
			mSignaler:Fire()
		end

		function sig:connect(f)
			if not f then error("connect(nil)", 2) end
			return mSignaler.Event:connect(function()
				f(unpack(mArgData, 1, mArgDataCount))
			end)
		end

		function sig:wait()
			mSignaler.Event:wait()
			assert(mArgData, "Missing arg data, likely due to :TweenSize/Position corrupting threadrefs.")
			return unpack(mArgData, 1, mArgDataCount)
		end

		return sig
	end

	function Util.DisconnectEvent(conn)
		if conn then
			conn:disconnect()
		end
		return nil
	end

	function Util.SetGUIInsetBounds(x, y)
		local success, _ = pcall(function() GuiService:SetGlobalGuiInset(0, x, 0, y) end)
		if not success then
			pcall(function() GuiService:SetGlobalSizeOffsetPixel(-x, -y) end) -- Legacy GUI-offset function
		end
	end

	local baseUrl = game:GetService("ContentProvider").BaseUrl:lower()
	baseUrl = string.gsub(baseUrl,"/m.","/www.") --mobile site does not work for this stuff!
	function Util.GetSecureApiBaseUrl()
		local secureApiUrl = baseUrl
		secureApiUrl = string.gsub(secureApiUrl,"http://","https://")
		secureApiUrl = string.gsub(secureApiUrl,"www","api")
		return secureApiUrl
	end

	function Util.GetPlayerByName(playerName)
		-- O(n), may be faster if I store a reverse hash from the players list; can't trust FindFirstChild in PlayersService because anything can be parented to there.
		local lowerName = string.lower(playerName)
		for _, player in pairs(PlayersService:GetPlayers()) do
			if string.lower(player.Name) == lowerName then
				return player
			end
		end
		return nil -- Found no player
	end

	local function MakeIsInGroup(groupId, requiredRank)
		assert(type(requiredRank) == "nil" or type(requiredRank) == "number", "requiredRank must be a number or nil")

		local inGroupCache = {}
		return function(player)
			if player and player.UserId then
				local userId = player.UserId

				if inGroupCache[userId] == nil then
					local inGroup = false
					pcall(function() -- Many things can error is the IsInGroup check
						if requiredRank then
							inGroup = player:GetRankInGroup(groupId) &gt; requiredRank
						else
							inGroup = player:IsInGroup(groupId)
						end
					end)
					inGroupCache[userId] = inGroup
				end

				return inGroupCache[userId]
			end

			return false
		end
	end
	Util.IsPlayerAdminAsync = MakeIsInGroup(1200769)
	Util.IsPlayerInternAsync = MakeIsInGroup(2868472, 100)

	local function GetNameValue(pName)
		local value = 0
		for index = 1, #pName do
			local cValue = string.byte(string.sub(pName, index, index))
			local reverseIndex = #pName - index + 1
			if #pName%2 == 1 then
				reverseIndex = reverseIndex - 1
			end
			if reverseIndex%4 &gt;= 2 then
				cValue = -cValue
			end
			value = value + cValue
		end
		return value
	end

	function Util.ComputeChatColor(pName)
		return CHAT_COLORS[(GetNameValue(pName) % #CHAT_COLORS) + 1]
	end

	-- This is a memo-izing function
	local testLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
	testLabel.TextWrapped = true;
	testLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
	testLabel.Parent = GuiRoot -- Note: We have to parent it to check TextBounds
	-- The TextSizeCache table looks like this Text-&gt;Font-&gt;sizeBounds-&gt;FontSize
	local TextSizeCache = {}
	function Util.GetStringTextBounds(text, font, fontSize, sizeBounds)
		-- If no sizeBounds are specified use some huge number
		sizeBounds = sizeBounds or false
		if not TextSizeCache[text] then
			TextSizeCache[text] = {}
		end
		if not TextSizeCache[text][font] then
			TextSizeCache[text][font] = {}
		end
		if not TextSizeCache[text][font][sizeBounds] then
			TextSizeCache[text][font][sizeBounds] = {}
		end
		if not TextSizeCache[text][font][sizeBounds][fontSize] then
			testLabel.Text = text
			testLabel.Font = font
			testLabel.FontSize = fontSize
			if sizeBounds then
				testLabel.TextWrapped = true;
				testLabel.Size = sizeBounds
			else
				testLabel.TextWrapped = false;
			end
			TextSizeCache[text][font][sizeBounds][fontSize] = testLabel.TextBounds
		end
		return TextSizeCache[text][font][sizeBounds][fontSize]
	end

	local PRINTABLE_CHARS = '[^' .. string.char(32) .. '-' ..  string.char(126) .. ']'
	local WHITESPACE_CHARS = '(' .. string.rep('%s', 7) .. ')%s+'
	function Util.FilterUnprintableCharacters(str)
		if not GetLuaChatFilteringFlag() then
			return str
		end

		local result = str:gsub(PRINTABLE_CHARS, '');
		result = str:gsub(WHITESPACE_CHARS, '%1');
		return result
	end
end

local SelectChatModeEvent = Util.Signal()
local SelectPlayerEvent = Util.Signal()

local function CreateChatMessage()
	local this = {}
	this.FadeRoutines = {}

	function this:GetMessageFontSize(settings)
		return Util.IsSmallScreenSize() and settings.SmallScreenFontSize or settings.FontSize
	end

	function this:OnResize()
		-- Nothing!
	end

	function this:FadeIn()
		local gui = this:GetGui()
		if gui then
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end

	function this:FadeOut()
		local gui = this:GetGui()
		if gui then
			gui.Visible = false
		end
	end

	function this:GetGui()
		return this.Container
	end

	function this:Destroy()
		if this.Container ~= nil then
			this.Container:Destroy()
			this.Container = nil
		end
		if this.FadeRoutines then
			for _, routine in pairs(this.FadeRoutines) do
				routine:Cancel()
			end
			this.FadeRoutines = {}
		end
	end

	return this
end

local function getYekuChatLocalization()
	local modulesRoot = script:FindFirstAncestor("Modules")
	local server = modulesRoot and modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("Server")
	local clientChat = server and server:FindFirstChild("ClientChat")
	local defaults = clientChat and clientChat:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	local moduleScript = defaults and defaults:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" and type(result.Get) == "function" then return result end
	return nil
end

local function resolveLocalizationMarkup(value)
	local text = tostring(value or "")
	local marker = "{RBX_LOCALIZATION_KEY}"
	local first = string.find(text, marker, 1, true)
	if not first then return text end
	local second = string.find(text, marker, first + #marker, true)
	if not second then return string.gsub(text, marker, "") end
	local keyPart = string.sub(text, first + #marker, second - 1)
	local fallbackPart = string.sub(text, second + #marker)
	local key = string.match(keyPart, "^%s*%((.-)%)%s*$") or string.match(keyPart, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
	local fallback = string.match(fallbackPart, "^%s*%((.*)%)%s*$") or fallbackPart
	key = string.gsub(tostring(key or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	fallback = string.gsub(tostring(fallback or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	if key == "" then return fallback end
	local localization = getYekuChatLocalization()
	if localization then
		local ok, translated = pcall(function() return localization:Get(key, fallback) end)
		if ok and type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" then return translated end
	end
	return fallback
end


local function CreateSystemChatMessage(settings, chattedMessage)
	local this = CreateChatMessage()

	this.Settings = settings
	this.rawChatString = resolveLocalizationMarkup(chattedMessage)

	function this:OnResize(containerSize)
		if this.Container and this.ChatMessage then

			if InputService.VREnabled then
				this.ChatMessage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
				this.ChatMessage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			end

			this.Container.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,1000)
			local textHeight = this.ChatMessage.TextBounds.Y

			local newContainerHeight = textHeight + 5
			this.Container.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,newContainerHeight)
			return newContainerHeight
		end
	end

	function this:FadeIn()
		local gui = this:GetGui()
		if gui then
			gui.Visible = true
			for _, routine in pairs(this.FadeRoutines) do
				routine:Cancel()
			end
			this.FadeRoutines = {}
			local tweenableObjects = {
				this.ChatMessage;
			}
			for _, object in pairs(tweenableObjects) do
				object.TextTransparency = 0;
				object.TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
			end

			if this.MessageBackgroundImage then
				this.MessageBackgroundImage.Visible = InputService.VREnabled
			end
		end
	end

	function this:FadeOut(instant)
		local gui = this:GetGui()
		if gui then
			if instant then
				gui.Visible = false
			else
				local tweenableObjects = {
					this.ChatMessage;
				}
				for _, object in pairs(tweenableObjects) do
					table.insert(this.FadeRoutines, Util.PropertyTweener(object, 'TextTransparency', object.TextTransparency, 1, 1, Util.Linear))
					table.insert(this.FadeRoutines, Util.PropertyTweener(object, 'TextStrokeTransparency', object.TextStrokeTransparency, 1, 0.85, Util.Linear))
				end
			end
			if this.MessageBackgroundImage then
				this.MessageBackgroundImage.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end

	local function CreateMessageGuiElement()
		local fontSize = this:GetMessageFontSize(this.Settings)

		local systemMessageDisplayText = this.rawChatString or ""
		local systemMessageSize = Util.GetStringTextBounds(systemMessageDisplayText, this.Settings.Font, fontSize, UDim2.new(0, 400, 0, 1000))

		local container = Util.Create'Frame'
		{
			Name = 'MessageContainer';
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0);
			ZIndex = 1;
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
		};
		this.MessageBackgroundImage = Util.Create'ImageLabel'
		{
			Name = 'TextEntryBackground';
			Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,-2);
			Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,1);
			Image = 'rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/VRChatBackground.png';
			ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice;
			SliceCenter = Rect.new(8,8,56,56);
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
			ImageTransparency = 0.3;
			BorderSizePixel = 0;
			ZIndex = 1;
			Visible = InputService.VREnabled;
			Parent = container;
		}

		local chatMessage = Util.Create'TextLabel'
		{
			Name = 'SystemChatMessage';
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0);
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0);
			Text = systemMessageDisplayText;
			ZIndex = 1;
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top;
			TextWrapped = true;
			TextColor3 = this.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor;
			FontSize = fontSize;
			Font = this.Settings.Font;
			TextStrokeColor3 = this.Settings.TextStrokeColor;
			TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
			Parent = container;
		};
		if InputService.VREnabled then
			chatMessage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
			chatMessage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		end

		container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, systemMessageSize.Y + 1);
		this.Container = container
		this.ChatMessage = chatMessage
	end

	CreateMessageGuiElement()

	return this
end

--[[ Popup Handling ]]--
function createPopupFrame(selectedPlayer, selectedButton)
	if selectedPlayer and selectedPlayer.Parent == PlayersService then
		if lastSelectedButton ~= selectedButton then
			if lastSelectedButton ~= nil then
				lastSelectedButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				lastSelectedButton = nil
			end
			lastSelectedButton = selectedButton
			lastSelectedPlayer = selectedPlayer
			selectedButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
		else
			lastSelectedPlayer = nil
		end
	end
end

function popupHidden()
	if lastSelectedButton then
		lastSelectedPlayer = nil
		lastSelectedButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lastSelectedButton = nil
	end
end

InputService.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject, isProcessed)
	if isProcessed then return end
	local inputType = inputObject.UserInputType
	if ((inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and
		inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin) or
		inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
	end
end)

--[[ End of popup handling ]]--

local function CreatePlayerChatMessage(settings, playerChatType, sendingPlayer, chattedMessage, receivingPlayer)
	local this = CreateChatMessage()

	this.Settings = settings
	this.PlayerChatType = playerChatType
	this.SendingPlayer = sendingPlayer
	this.RawMessageContent = resolveLocalizationMarkup(chattedMessage)
	this.ReceivingPlayer = receivingPlayer
	this.ReceivedTime = tick()

	this.Neutral = this.SendingPlayer and this.SendingPlayer.Neutral or true
	this.TeamColor = this.SendingPlayer and this.SendingPlayer.TeamColor or BrickColor.new("White")

	function this:OnResize(containerSize)
		if this.Container and this.ChatMessage then
			this.Container.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,1000)
			local textHeight = this.ChatMessage.TextBounds.Y
			local newContainerHeight = textHeight + 5
			this.Container.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,newContainerHeight)
			return newContainerHeight
		end
	end

	function this:FormatMessage()
		local result = ""
		if this.RawMessageContent then
			local message = this.RawMessageContent
			result = message
		end
		return result
	end

	function this:FormatChatType()
		if this.PlayerChatType then
			if this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.All then
				--return "[All]"
			elseif this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Team then
				return "[Team]"
			elseif this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper then
				-- nothing!
			end
		end
	end

	function this:FormatPlayerNameText()
		local playerName = ""
		-- If we are sending a whisper to someone, then we should show their name
		if this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper and this.SendingPlayer and this.SendingPlayer == Player then
			playerName = (this.ReceivingPlayer and this.ReceivingPlayer.Name or "")
		else
			playerName = (this.SendingPlayer and this.SendingPlayer.Name or "")
		end
		return "[" ..  playerName .. "]:"
	end

	function this:FadeIn()
		local gui = this:GetGui()
		if gui then
			gui.Visible = true
			for _, routine in pairs(this.FadeRoutines) do
				routine:Cancel()
			end
			this.FadeRoutines = {}
			local tweenableObjects = {
				this.WhisperToText;
				this.WhisperFromText;
				this.ChatModeButton;
				this.UserNameButton;
				this.ChatMessage;
			}
			for _, object in pairs(tweenableObjects) do
				object.TextTransparency = 0;
				object.TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
				object.Active = true
			end
			if this.UserNameDot then
				this.UserNameDot.ImageTransparency = 0
			end

			if this.MessageBackgroundImage then
				this.MessageBackgroundImage.Visible = InputService.VREnabled
			end
		end
	end

	function this:FadeOut(instant)
		local gui = this:GetGui()
		if gui then
			if instant then
				gui.Visible = false
			else
				local tweenableObjects = {
					this.WhisperToText;
					this.WhisperFromText;
					this.ChatModeButton;
					this.UserNameButton;
					this.ChatMessage;
				}
				for _, object in pairs(tweenableObjects) do
					table.insert(this.FadeRoutines, Util.PropertyTweener(object, 'TextTransparency', object.TextTransparency, 1, 1, Util.Linear))
					table.insert(this.FadeRoutines, Util.PropertyTweener(object, 'TextStrokeTransparency', object.TextStrokeTransparency, 1, 0.85, Util.Linear))
					object.Active = false
				end
				if this.UserNameDot then
					table.insert(this.FadeRoutines, Util.PropertyTweener(this.UserNameDot, 'ImageTransparency', this.UserNameDot.ImageTransparency, 1, 1, Util.Linear))
				end
			end
			if this.MessageBackgroundImage then
				this.MessageBackgroundImage.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end

	function this:Destroy()
		if this.Container ~= nil then
			this.Container:Destroy()
			this.Container = nil
		end
		this.ClickedOnModeConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.ClickedOnModeConn)
		this.ClickedOnPlayerConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.ClickedOnPlayerConn)
	end

	local function CreateMessageGuiElement()
		local fontSize = this:GetMessageFontSize(this.Settings)

		local toMesasgeDisplayText = "To "
		local toMessageSize = Util.GetStringTextBounds(toMesasgeDisplayText, this.Settings.Font, fontSize)
		local fromMesasgeDisplayText = "From "
		local fromMessageSize = Util.GetStringTextBounds(fromMesasgeDisplayText, this.Settings.Font, fontSize)
		local chatTypeDisplayText = this:FormatChatType()
		local chatTypeSize = chatTypeDisplayText and Util.GetStringTextBounds(chatTypeDisplayText, this.Settings.Font, fontSize) or Vector2.new(0,0)
		local playerNameDisplayText = this:FormatPlayerNameText()
		local playerNameSize = Util.GetStringTextBounds(playerNameDisplayText, this.Settings.Font, fontSize)

		local singleSpaceSize = Util.GetStringTextBounds(" ", this.Settings.Font, fontSize)
		local numNeededSpaces = math.ceil(playerNameSize.X / singleSpaceSize.X) + 3 -- V36: add two measured spaces between name and message
		local chatMessageDisplayText = string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces) .. this:FormatMessage()
		local chatMessageSize = Util.GetStringTextBounds(chatMessageDisplayText, this.Settings.Font, fontSize, UDim2.new(0, 400 - 5 - playerNameSize.X, 0, 1000))


		local playerColor = this.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor
		if this.SendingPlayer then
			if this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper then
				if this.SendingPlayer == Player and this.ReceivingPlayer then
					playerColor = Util.ComputeChatColor(this.ReceivingPlayer.Name)
				else
					playerColor = Util.ComputeChatColor(this.SendingPlayer.Name)
				end
			else
				if this.SendingPlayer.Neutral then
					playerColor = Util.ComputeChatColor(this.SendingPlayer.Name)
				else
					playerColor = this.SendingPlayer.TeamColor.Color
				end
			end
		end

		local container = Util.Create'Frame'
		{
			Name = 'MessageContainer';
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0);
			ZIndex = 1;
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
		};
		this.MessageBackgroundImage = Util.Create'ImageLabel'
		{
			Name = 'TextEntryBackground';
			Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,-2);
			Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,1);
			Image = 'rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/VRChatBackground.png';
			ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice;
			SliceCenter = Rect.new(8,8,56,56);
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
			ImageTransparency = 0.3;
			BorderSizePixel = 0;
			ZIndex = 1;
			Visible = InputService.VREnabled;
			Parent = container;
		}

		local xOffset = InputService.VREnabled and 4 or 0

		if this.SendingPlayer and this.SendingPlayer == Player and this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper then
			local whisperToText = Util.Create'TextLabel'
			{
				Name = 'WhisperTo';
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0);
				Size = UDim2.new(0, toMessageSize.X, 0, toMessageSize.Y);
				Text = toMesasgeDisplayText;
				ZIndex = 1;
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
				BackgroundTransparency = 1;
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
				TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top;
				TextWrapped = true;
				TextColor3 = this.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor;
				FontSize = fontSize;
				Font = this.Settings.Font;
				TextStrokeColor3 = this.Settings.TextStrokeColor;
				TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
				Parent = container;
			};
			xOffset = xOffset + toMessageSize.X
			this.WhisperToText = whisperToText
		elseif this.SendingPlayer and this.SendingPlayer ~= Player and this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper then
			local whisperFromText = Util.Create'TextLabel'
			{
				Name = 'WhisperFromText';
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0);
				Size = UDim2.new(0, fromMessageSize.X, 0, fromMessageSize.Y);
				Text = fromMesasgeDisplayText;
				ZIndex = 1;
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
				BackgroundTransparency = 1;
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
				TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top;
				TextWrapped = true;
				TextColor3 = this.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor;
				FontSize = fontSize;
				Font = this.Settings.Font;
				TextStrokeColor3 = this.Settings.TextStrokeColor;
				TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
				Parent = container;
			};
			xOffset = xOffset + fromMessageSize.X
			this.WhisperFromText = whisperFromText
		end
		if chatTypeDisplayText then
			local chatModeButton = Util.Create(Util.IsTouchDevice() and 'TextLabel' or 'TextButton')
			{
				Name = 'ChatMode';
				BackgroundTransparency = 1;
				ZIndex = 2;
				Text = chatTypeDisplayText;
				TextColor3 = this.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor;
				Position = UDim2.new(0, xOffset, 0, 0);
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
				TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top;
				FontSize = fontSize;
				Font = this.Settings.Font;
				Size = UDim2.new(0, chatTypeSize.X, 0, chatTypeSize.Y);
				TextStrokeColor3 = this.Settings.TextStrokeColor;
				TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
				Parent = container
			}
			if chatModeButton:IsA('TextButton') then
				this.ClickedOnModeConn = chatModeButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					SelectChatModeEvent:fire(this.PlayerChatType)
				end)
			end
			if this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Team then
				chatModeButton.TextColor3 = playerColor
			end
			xOffset = xOffset + chatTypeSize.X + 1
			this.ChatModeButton = chatModeButton
		end
		local userNameButton = Util.Create(Util.IsTouchDevice() and 'TextLabel' or 'TextButton')
		{
			Name = 'PlayerName';
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 1);
			BorderSizePixel = 0;
			ZIndex = 2;
			Text = playerNameDisplayText;
			TextColor3 = playerColor;
			Position = UDim2.new(0, xOffset, 0, 0);
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top;
			FontSize = fontSize;
			Font = this.Settings.Font;
			Size = UDim2.new(0, playerNameSize.X, 0, playerNameSize.Y);
			TextStrokeColor3 = this.Settings.TextStrokeColor;
			TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
			Parent = container
		}
		if userNameButton:IsA('TextButton') then
			this.ClickedOnPlayerConn = userNameButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
				local gui = this:GetGui()
				if gui and gui.Visible then
					if this.PlayerChatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper and this.SendingPlayer == Player and this.ReceivingPlayer then
						SelectPlayerEvent:fire(this.ReceivingPlayer)
					else
						SelectPlayerEvent:fire(this.SendingPlayer)
					end
				end
			end)

		end

		local chatMessage = Util.Create'TextLabel'
		{
			Name = 'ChatMessage';
			Position = UDim2.new(0, xOffset, 0, 0);
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -xOffset, 1, 0);
			Text = chatMessageDisplayText;
			ZIndex = 1;
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
			BackgroundTransparency = 1;
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top;
			TextWrapped = true;
			TextColor3 = this.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor;
			FontSize = fontSize;
			Font = this.Settings.Font;
			TextStrokeColor3 = this.Settings.TextStrokeColor;
			TextStrokeTransparency = this.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency;
			Parent = container;
		};
		if InputService.VREnabled then
			chatMessage.Size = chatMessage.Size - UDim2.new(0,4,0,0)
		end
		-- Check if they got moderated and put up a real message instead of Label
		if chatMessage.Text == 'Label' and chatMessageDisplayText ~= 'Label' then
			chatMessage.Text = string.rep(" ", numNeededSpaces) .. '[Content Deleted]'
		end
		if this.SendingPlayer then
			if PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerAdminAsync(this.SendingPlayer) then
				chatMessage.TextColor3 = this.Settings.AdminTextColor
			elseif PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerInternAsync(this.SendingPlayer) then
				chatMessage.TextColor3 = this.Settings.InternTextColor
			end
		end
		chatMessage.Size = chatMessage.Size + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, chatMessage.TextBounds.Y);

		container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.max(chatMessageSize.Y + 1, userNameButton.Size.Y.Offset + 1));
		this.Container = container
		this.ChatMessage = chatMessage
		this.UserNameButton = userNameButton
	end

	CreateMessageGuiElement()

	return this
end

local function CreateChatBarWidget(settings)
	local this = {}

	-- MessageModes: {All, Team, Whisper}
	this.MessageMode = 'All'
	this.TargetWhisperPlayer = nil
	this.Settings = settings

	this.WidgetVisible = false
	this.FadedIn = true

	this.ChatBarGainedFocusEvent = Util.Signal()
	this.ChatBarLostFocusEvent = Util.Signal()
	this.ChatCommandEvent = Util.Signal() -- Signal Signatue: success, actionType, [captures]
	this.ChatErrorEvent = Util.Signal() -- Signal Signatue: success, actionType, [captures]
	this.ChatBarFloodEvent = Util.Signal()

	this.unfocusedAt = 0

	local chatCoreGuiEnabled = true

	-- This function while lets string.find work case-insensitively without clobbering the case of the captures
	local function nocase(s)
		s = string.gsub(s, "%a", function (c)
			return string.format("[%s%s]", string.lower(c),
				string.upper(c))
		end)
		return s
	end

	this.ChatMatchingRegex =
		{
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/w ") .. "(%w+_?%w+)") end] = "Whisper";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/whisper ") .. "(%w+_?%w+)") end] = "Whisper";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, "^%%") end] = "Team";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, "^%(TEAM%)") end] = "Team";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/t")) end] = "Team";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/team")) end] = "Team";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/a")) end] = "All";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/all")) end] = "All";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/s")) end] = "All";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/say")) end] = "All";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/e")) end] = "Emote";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/emote")) end] = "Emote";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, "^/%?") end] = "Help";
			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/help")) end] = "Help";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/block ") .. "(%w+_?%w+)") end] = "Block";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/unblock ") .. "(%w+_?%w+)") end] = "Unblock";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/mute ") .. "(%w+_?%w+)") end] = "Mute";

			[function(chatBarText) return string.find(chatBarText, nocase("^/unmute ") .. "(%w+_?%w+)") end] = "Unmute";
		}

	local ChatModesDict =
		{
			['Whisper'] = 'Whisper';
			['Team'] = 'Team';
			['All'] = 'All';
			[Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper] = 'Whisper';
			[Enum.PlayerChatType.Team] = 'Team';
			[Enum.PlayerChatType.All] = 'All';
		}

	local function TearDownEvents()
		this.ClickToChatButtonConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.ClickToChatButtonConn)
		this.ChatBarFocusLostConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.ChatBarFocusLostConn)
		this.ChatBarLostFocusConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.ChatBarLostFocusConn)
		this.SelectChatModeConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.SelectChatModeConn)
		this.SelectPlayerConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.SelectPlayerConn)
		this.FocusChatBarInputBeganConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.FocusChatBarInputBeganConn)
		this.InputBeganConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.InputBeganConn)
		this.ChatBarChangedConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.ChatBarChangedConn)
	end

	local function HookUpEvents()
		TearDownEvents() -- Cleanup old events

		if this.ClickToChatButton then this.ClickToChatButtonConn = this.ClickToChatButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function() this:FocusChatBar() end) end

		if this.ChatBar then
			-- Use a count to check for double backspace out of a chatmode
			local count = 0
			if not Util.IsTouchDevice() then
				this.FocusChatBarInputBeganConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.FocusChatBarInputBeganConn)
				this.FocusChatBarInputBeganConn = InputService.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObj)
					if inputObj.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace and this:GetChatBarText() == "" then
						if count == 0 then
							count = count + 1
						else
							this:SetMessageMode('All')
						end
					else
						count = 0
					end
				end)
			end

			this.ChatBarFocusLostConn = this.ChatBar.FocusLost:connect(function(...)
				count = 0
				this.unfocusedAt = tick()
				this.ChatBarLostFocusEvent:fire(...)
			end)
			this.ChatBarChangedConn = this.ChatBar.Changed:connect(function(prop)
				if prop == "Text" then
					this:OnChatBarTextChanged()
				elseif prop == 'TextFits' or prop == 'TextBounds' or prop == 'Visible' then
					this:OnChatBarBoundsChanged()
				end
			end)
		end

		if this.ChatBarLostFocusEvent then this.ChatBarLostFocusConn = this.ChatBarLostFocusEvent:connect(function(...) this:OnChatBarFocusLost(...) end) end

		this.SelectChatModeConn = SelectChatModeEvent:connect(function(chatType)
			this:SetMessageMode(chatType)
			this:FocusChatBar()
		end)

		this.SelectPlayerConn = SelectPlayerEvent:connect(function(chatPlayer)
			this.TargetWhisperPlayer = chatPlayer
			this:SetMessageMode("Whisper")
			this:FocusChatBar()
		end)

		this.InputBeganConn = InputService.InputBegan:connect(function(inputObject)
			if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
				-- Clear text when they press escape
				this:SetChatBarText("")
			end
		end)
	end

	function this:CalculateVisibility()
		if this.ChatBarContainer then
			local enabled = self.WidgetVisible and chatCoreGuiEnabled and not NON_CORESCRIPT_MODE
			if enabled then
				HookUpEvents()
			else
				TearDownEvents()
			end
			this.ChatBarContainer.Visible = enabled and self.FadedIn and (not chatBarDisabled)
		end
	end

	function this:ToggleVisibility(visible)
		if visible ~= self.WidgetVisible then
			self.WidgetVisible = visible
			self:CalculateVisibility()
		end
		if NON_CORESCRIPT_MODE or chatBarDisabled then
			this.ChatBarContainer.Visible = false
		end
	end

	function this:FadeIn()
		self.FadedIn = true
		self:CalculateVisibility()
	end

	function this:FadeOut()
		self.FadedIn = false
		self:CalculateVisibility()
	end
